### PART 3A — Temporary Building and Changes of Use for Public Health Emergency Purposes[^c-90775f749d1e4aac1be0b64f2373f07b]

#### Class A

##### A. Permitted development

**Development for the purpose of preventing, controlling or mitigating the effects of, or taking other action in connection with a public health emergency in the United Kingdom, consisting of—**

- (a) **a change of the use of a building or land to a use falling within Class C2 (Residential institutions) or Class D1 (Non-residential institutions) of the Schedule to the Use Classes Order,**
- (b) **the provision on land of buildings, moveable structures, works, plant or machinery.**

##### A.1. Development not permitted

Development is not permitted by Class A if—

- (a) any part of the development is on land which is, or forms part of—
  - (i) a military explosive storage area;
  - (ii) a safety hazard area;
  - (iii) a site of special scientific interest; or
- (b) the land or building is, or contains, a scheduled monument.

##### A.2. Conditions

Development is permitted by Class A subject to the following conditions—

- (a) the development must be undertaken by or on behalf of an NHS body;
- (b) if the developer is not also the local planning authority, the developer must, as soon as reasonably practicable notify the local planning authority of that development; [^c-1aaf46ebce611cb7a54350ff2b43ccd7]...
- (c)[^c-bfcebad7848a330531d250c555247f2f] on or before the expiry date—
  - (i) any use of that building or land for the purpose of Class A must cease;
  - (ii) any building, moveable structure, works, plant or machinery permitted by Class A must be removed; and
  - (iii) the building or land must be restored to its condition before the development took place, or to such other state as may be agreed in writing between the local planning authority and the developer; and
- (d)[^c-0ed38d8200d9853c1372437cc00272c5] in sub-paragraph (c), “the expiry date” means—
  - (i) for developments which begin before 10 April 2021 the date eighteen months after the date on which the development began; and
  - (ii) for developments which begin on or after 10 April 2021 the date twelve months after the date on which the development began.

##### A.3. Interpretation of Class A

(1) For the purposes of Class A—

- “military explosives storage area” means any area, including an aerodrome, depot, mooring or port, at which the storage of military explosives may be undertaken and for which the associated explosives safeguarding zone is identified on a safeguarding map, issued by the Secretary of State provided to the local planning authority for the purposes of a direction made by the Welsh Ministers in exercise of powers conferred by article 18(1) of the Procedure Order (or any previous powers to the like effect);
- “NHS body” has the same meaning as in section 206 of the National Health Service (Wales) Act 2006;
- “Procedure Order” means the Town and Country Planning (Development Management Procedure) (Wales) Order 2012;
- “public health emergency” means an event or situation which threatens serious damage to human welfare in a place in the United Kingdom;
- “safety hazard area” means an area notified to the local planning authority—
  - (a) by the Health and Safety Executive for the purposes of paragraph (c) of the Table in Schedule 4 to the Procedure Order (or any previous powers to the like effect); or
  - (b) by the Office for Nuclear Regulation for the purposes of paragraph (d) of that Table.

(2) For the purposes of the definition of “public health emergency” in paragraph (1), an event or situation threatens damage to human welfare only if it involves, causes or may cause—

- (a) loss of human life;
- (b) human illness or injury;
- (c) disruption of services relating to health.

### PART 4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — TEMPORARY BUILDINGS AND USES (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the operations referred to are mining operations, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] planning permission is required for those operations but is not granted or deemed to be granted. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class A subject to the conditions that, when the operations have been carried out— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any building, structure, works, plant or machinery permitted by Class A shall be removed, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any adjoining land on which development permitted by Class A has been carried out shall, as soon as reasonably practicable, be reinstated to its condition before that development was carried out. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ce631cd1390718215aff5c1b52414618][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the use of the land is for the siting of any caravan except a caravan which— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] is a motor vehicle designed or adapted for human habitation, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] is sited on the land in connection with a festival, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the land is, or is within, a site of special scientific interest and the use of the land is for— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a purpose referred to in paragraph B.2(b) or other motor sports; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any war game, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

[^c-d67626fed2a517da3b576921a9e16ef3]... *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the use of the land is for the display of an advertisement *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] , or[^c-67df726aa8fd45b8b12dd6c20d482941] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Interpretation of Class B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

The purposes mentioned in Class B above are— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the holding of a market; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] motor car and motorcycle racing including trials of speed, and practising for these activities. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).3**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] In Class B, “war game" means an enacted, mock or imaginary battle conducted with weapons which are designed not to injure (including smoke bombs, or guns or grenades which fire or spray paint or are otherwise used to mark other participants), but excludes military activities or training exercises organised by or with the authority of the Secretary of State for Defence. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class BA (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b56f7907827eccc878f46869e4f004d][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### BA (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development consisting of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the use of any land, other than that described in sub-paragraph (b), as a recreational campsite, for not more than 60 days in total in any calendar year; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the use of any land within a National Park, an area of outstanding natural beauty, or a World Heritage Site as a recreational campsite, for not more than 28 days in total in any calendar year; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the provision on land described in sub-paragraphs (a) and (b) of any moveable structure reasonably necessary for the purposes of the permitted use of that land. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] on a site in a special area of conservation; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### BA2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Conditions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class BA subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the developer must, before beginning the development in each calendar year, apply to the local planning authority in accordance with the procedure set out in paragraph BA.3, for a determination as to whether the prior approval of the authority is required as to— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] arrangements for toilet and wastewater facilities, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] arrangements for waste disposal facilities, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the proposed means of vehicular access to the site at which the development is to be carried out; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where the local planning authority has given the developer written notice under paragraph BA.3 that prior approval is required, the developer must, before beginning the development, apply to the local planning authority for prior approval under the procedure in paragraph BA.4; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development must not begin before the occurrence of one of the following— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the receipt by the developer of the local planning authority’s written notice of their determination that prior approval is not required; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the expiry of 28 days following the date on which the application for a determination made under paragraph BA3 was received by the local planning authority without the local planning authority— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (aa (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] notifying the developer that the application does not comply with paragraph BA.3(a), or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (bb (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] notifying the developer of their determination; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where a developer is required to apply to the local planning authority for prior approval as a result of a determination under paragraph BA.3— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (aa (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the developer has made an application under paragraph BA.4, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (bb (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the developer has received the local planning authority’s written notice that prior approval has been granted; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development must be carried out— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where prior approval is required, in accordance with the details approved by the local planning authority; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where prior approval is not required, in accordance with the site plan and particulars submitted by the developer under paragraph BA.3(a). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] waste disposal facilities, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the siting of pitches and amenities, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iv (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] details of access from the highway, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (v (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the dates on which the site will be in use; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] an application for determination must be accompanied by any fee required to be paid; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the local planning authority must provide the developer within 28 days of the receipt of the application for determination with either— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] written notice of the local planning authority’s determination, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] notice in the form required by section 62ZA of the Act that the application does not comply with paragraph (a) or (b); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where, in the opinion of the local planning authority, the development is likely to result in a material increase, or a material change in the character of traffic in the vicinity of the site to be developed, the local planning authority must determine that the developer must apply for prior approval. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where the application relates to prior approval as to transport and highways impacts of the development, on receipt of the application, if, in the opinion of the local planning authority the development is likely to result in a material increase or a material change in the character of traffic in the vicinity of the site, the local planning authority must consult— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the highway authority for the trunk road, where the increase or change relates to traffic entering or leaving a trunk road, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the local highway authority, where the increase or change relates to traffic entering or leaving a classified road or proposed highway, except where the local planning authority is the local highway authority, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the operator of the network which includes or consists of the railway in question, and the Welsh Ministers, where the increase or change relates to traffic using a level crossing over a railway; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] before granting prior approval on an application made under this paragraph the local planning authority may consult authorities or persons falling within a category set out in the table in Schedule 4 to the Town and Country Planning (Development Management Procedure) (Wales) Order 2012; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where a local planning authority consults any authority or person (“ *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**the consultee *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**”) under sub-paragraph (c), the authority must not determine the application until at least 21 days after the date on which notice is given to the consultee; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the local planning authority must, in determining the application, have due regard to any representations received from a consultee; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the local planning authority may grant prior approval unconditionally or subject to conditions reasonably related to the subject matter requiring prior approval. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

### PART 4A — TEMPORARY CHANGES OF USE[^c-b2cf29963a4028b1053f97e43ce88de6]

#### Class A — Additional temporary use of land during the relevant period

##### A. Permitted Development

**The use of any land for any purposes for not more than 28 days in total during the relevant period, of which no more than 14 days in total may be for the purposes of—**

- (a) **the holding of a market;**
- (b) **motor car and motorcycle racing including trials of speed, and practising for these activities,**

**and the provision on the land of any moveable structure for the purposes of the permitted use.**

**In calculating the number of days during which development is permitted, no account is to be taken of any day during which development is permitted under Class B of Part 4, or Class B of Part 4A, of this Schedule.**

##### A.1. Development not permitted

Development is not permitted by Class A if—

- (a) the land in question is a building;
- (b) the land is within the curtilage of a building and there is a scheduled monument within that curtilage;
- (c) the land is within a National Park and the use of the land is for a car park that is not ancillary to a temporary use under Class A of this part;
- (d) the use of the land is for a caravan site;
- (e) the land is, or is within, a site of special scientific interest or is within the curtilage of a listed building and the use of the land is for—
  - (i) motor car and motorcycle racing including trials of speed or other motor sports, and practising for these activities;
  - (ii) clay pigeon shooting;
  - (iii) any war game;
- (f) the use of the land is for the display of an advertisement.

##### A.2. Interpretation of Class A

For the purposes of Class A—

- “the relevant period” means the period beginning on 30 April 2021 and ending on 3 January 2022;
- “war game” means an enacted, mock or imaginary battle conducted with weapons which are designed not to injure (including smoke bombs, or guns or grenades which fire or spray paint or are otherwise used to mark other participants), but excludes military activities or training exercises organised by or with the authority of the Secretary of State for Defence.

#### Class B — Holding of a market by or on behalf of a local authority

##### B. Permitted Development

**The use of any land for the purposes of holding a market by or on behalf of a local authority during the relevant period and the provision on the land of any moveable structure for the permitted use.**

##### B.1. Development not permitted

Development is not permitted by Class B if the land is, or is within, a site of special scientific interest.

##### B.2. Interpretation of Class B

For the purposes of Class B “the relevant period” means the period beginning on 30 April 2021 and ending on 3 January 2022.

#### Class C — Temporary uses – Class A1 buildings in town centres

##### C. Permitted Development

**Development consisting of a change of use of a building—**

- (a) **during the relevant period, from a use falling within Class A1 (shops) of Schedule 1 to the Use Classes Order to a use falling within the following classes of Schedule 1 to the Use Classes Order—**
  - (i) **A2 (financial and professional services);**
  - (ii) **A3 (food and drink);**
  - (iii) **B1 (business);**
  - (iv) **D1 (non-residential institutions);**
  - (v) **D2 (assembly and leisure).**
- (b) **during or at the end of the six month period beginning with the date on which the development began, from the new use to the original use;**
- (c) **at the end of the relevant period, from the new use to the original use.**

##### C.1. Development not permitted

Development is not permitted by Class C if—

- (a) the whole of the building does not fall within a town centre;
- (b) the proposed A3 use is the sale of hot food for consumption off the premises;
- (c) the proposed use is Class B1(c) of Schedule 1 to the Use Classes Order.

##### C.2. Conditions

Development is permitted by Class C subject to the following conditions—

- (a) the developer must, as soon as reasonably practicable, notify the local planning authority of the development;
- (b) the change of use may last no more than six months beginning with the date on which the development began;
- (c) the change of use must end on or before the end of the relevant period.

##### C.3. Interpretation of Class C

For the purposes of Class C “the relevant period” means the period beginning on 30 April 2021 and ending on 29 April 2022.

#### Class D — Temporary uses – Class A2 buildings in town centres

##### D. Permitted Development

**Development consisting of a change of use of a building—**

- (a) **during the relevant period, from a use falling within Class A2 (financial and professional services) of Schedule 1 to the Use Classes Order to a use falling within the following classes of Schedule 1 to the Use Classes Order—**
  - (i) **A1 (shops);**
  - (ii) **A3 (food and drink);**
  - (iii) **B1 (business);**
  - (iv) **D1 (non-residential institutions);**
  - (v) **D2 (assembly and leisure);**
- (b) **during or at the end of the six month period beginning with the date on which the development began, from the new use the original use;**
- (c) **at the end of the relevant period, from the new use to the original use.**

##### D.1. Development not permitted

Development is not permitted by Class D if—

- (a) the whole of the building does not fall within a town centre;
- (b) the proposed use is sale of hot food for consumption off the premises;
- (c) the proposed use is Class B1(c) of Schedule 1 to the Use Classes Order.

##### D.2. Conditions

Development is permitted by Class D subject to the following conditions—

- (a) the developer must, as soon as reasonably practicable, notify the local planning authority of the development;
- (b) the change of use may last no more than six months beginning with the date on which the development began;
- (c) the change of use must end on or before the end of the relevant period.

##### D.3. Interpretation of Class D

For the purposes of Class D “the relevant period” means the period beginning on 30 April 2021 and ending on 29 April 2022.

#### Class E — Temporary uses – Class A3 buildings in town centres

##### E. Permitted Development

**E. Development consisting of a change of use of a building—**

- (a) **during the relevant period, from a use falling within Class A3 (food and drink) of Schedule 1 to the Use Classes Order to a use falling within the following classes of Schedule 1 to the Use Classes Order—**
  - (i) **A1 (shops);**
  - (ii) **A2 (financial and professional services);**
  - (iii) **B1 (business);**
  - (iv) **D1 (non-residential institutions);**
  - (v) **D2 (assembly and leisure);**
- (b) **during or at the end of the six month period beginning with the date on which the development began, from the new use to the original use;**
- (c) **at the end of the relevant period, from the new use to the original use.**

##### E.1. Development not permitted

Development is not permitted by Class E if—

- (a) the whole of the building does not fall within a town centre;
- (b) the proposed use falls within Class B1(c) of Schedule 1 to the Use Classes Order.

##### E.2. Conditions

Development is permitted by Class E subject to the following conditions—

- (a) the developer must, as soon as reasonably practicable, notify the local planning authority of the development;
- (b) the change of use may last no more than six months beginning with the date on which the development began;
- (c) the change of use must end on or before the end of the relevant period.

##### E.3. Interpretation of Class E

For the purposes of Class E “the relevant period” means the period beginning on 30 April 2021 and ending on 29 April 2022.

#### Class F — Hospitality uses - outdoor servery provision

##### F. Permitted Development

**The use, during the relevant period, of part of a relevant highway adjacent to premises falling within Class A3 (food and drink) of Schedule 1 to the Use Classes Order for the purposes of placing removable furniture, used for the purposes of selling or serving food or drink supplied from those premises or consuming food or drink supplied from those premises.**

##### F.1. Conditions

Development is permitted by Class F subject to the following conditions—

- (a) permission for the use of the part of the relevant highway must have been obtained from the relevant council under section 115E of the Highways Act 1980;
- (b) the use must be in accordance with any conditions imposed by the relevant council under section 115F of the Highways Act 1980;
- (c) the removable furniture must not be used between 10 pm and 8 am.

##### F.2. Interpretation of Class F

For the purposes of Class F—

- “furniture” means—
  - (a) counters or stalls for selling or serving food or drink,
  - (b) tables, counters or shelves on which food or drink can be placed,
  - (c) chairs, benches or other forms of seating, and
  - (d) umbrellas, barriers, heaters and other articles used in connection with the outdoor consumption of food or drink;
- “relevant highway” means—
  - (a) a highway to which Part 7A of the Highways Act 1980 applies, and
  - (b) which is not over Crown land or maintained by Network Rail;
- “the relevant period” means the period beginning on 30 April 2021 and ending on 3 January 2022.

##### G. Interpretation of Part 4A

For the purposes of Part 4A—

- “within a town centre” means within an area identified in a development plan as a—
  - (a) city centre shopping area;
  - (b) central shopping area;
  - (c) central shopping centre;
  - (d) district centre;
  - (e) district, local, village or neighbourhood centre;
  - (f) district shopping centre;
  - (g) local centre;
  - (h) principal town centre boundary;
  - (i) shopping area;
  - (j) shopping zone;
  - (k) Swansea central area;
  - (l) town and district retail centre;
  - (m) town centre;
  - (n) town centre area;
  - (o) town centre boundary;
  - (p) town/district centre boundary;
  - (q) retail area;
  - (r) retail and commercial hierarchy; or
  - (s) retail centre;
- “development plan” has the same meaning as in section 336 of the Act.

### PART 6 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — AGRICULTURAL BUILDINGS AND OPERATIONS (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class A Development on units of 5 hectares or more (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**The carrying out on agricultural land comprised in an agricultural unit of 5 hectares or more in area of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **works for the erection, extension or alteration of a building; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **any excavation or engineering operations, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

**which are reasonably necessary for the purposes of agriculture within that unit. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class A if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development would be carried out on a separate parcel of land forming part of the unit which is less than 1 hectare in area; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would consist of, or include, the erection, extension or alteration of a dwelling; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would involve the provision of a building, structure or works not designed for agricultural purposes; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the ground area which would be covered by— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any works or structure (other than a fence) for accommodating livestock or any plant or machinery arising from engineering operations; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any building erected or extended or altered by virtue of Class A, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  would exceed 465 square metres, calculated as described in paragraph D.2 below; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height of any part of any building, structure or works within 3 kilometres of the perimeter of an aerodrome would exceed 3 metres; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height of any part of any building, structure or works not within 3 kilometres of the perimeter of an aerodrome would exceed 12 metres; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (g (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any part of the development would be within 25 metres of a metalled part of a trunk road or classified road; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (h (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would consist of, or include, the erection or construction of, or the carrying out of any works to, a building, structure or an excavation used or to be used for the accommodation of livestock or for the storage of slurry or sewage sludge where the building, structure or excavation is, or would be, within 400 metres of the curtilage of a protected building; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c-8b811cb7d8382688bd26c77642316d6c]... *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would involve excavations or engineering operations on or over article 1(6) land which are connected with fish farming *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] ; or[^c-c2fe833ee6a678d1225c904bb15bf4e3] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (j (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-1dc779483a33671341d16aac75ddb6fb][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any building for storing fuel for or waste from a biomass boiler or an anaerobic digestion system— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] would be used for storing waste not produced by that boiler or system or for storing fuel not produced on land within the unit; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] is or would be within 400 metres of the curtilage of a protected building. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Conditions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Development is permitted by Class A subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where development is carried out within 400 metres of the curtilage of a protected building, any building, structure, excavation or works resulting from the development shall not be used for the accommodation of livestock except in the circumstances described in paragraph D.3 below or for the storage of slurry or sewage sludge *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] , for housing a biomass boiler or an anaerobic digestion system, for storage of fuel or waste from that boiler or system, or for housing a hydro-turbine;[^c-69acc40d365184e3147198fba2005945] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where the development involves— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the extraction of any mineral from the land (including removal from any disused railway embankment); or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the removal of any mineral from a mineral-working deposit, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  the mineral shall not be moved off the unit; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] waste materials shall not be brought on to the land from elsewhere for deposit except for use in works described in Class A(a) or in the provision of a hard surface and any materials so brought shall be incorporated forthwith into the building or works in question. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Subject to paragraph (3), development consisting of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the erection, extension or alteration of a building; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the formation or alteration of a private way; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the carrying out of excavations or the deposit of waste material (where the relevant area, as defined in paragraph D.4 below, exceeds 0.5 hectare); or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the placing or assembly of a tank in any waters, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

is permitted by Class A subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the developer shall, before beginning the development, apply to the local planning authority for a determination as to whether the prior approval of the authority will be required to the siting, design and external appearance of the building, the siting and means of construction of the private way, the siting of the excavation or deposit or the siting and appearance of the tank, as the case may be; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the application shall be accompanied by a written description of the proposed development and of the materials to be used and a plan indicating the site together with any fee required to be paid; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development shall not be begun before the occurrence of one of the following— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (aa (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the receipt by the applicant from the local planning authority of a written notice of their determination that such prior approval is not required; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (bb (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where the local planning authority give the applicant notice within 28 days following the date of receiving his application of their determination that such prior approval is required, the giving of such approval; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (cc (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the expiry of 28 days following the date on which the application was received by the local planning authority without the local planning authority making any determination as to whether such approval is required or notifying the applicant of their determination; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (iv (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (aa (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where the local planning authority give the applicant notice that such prior approval is required the applicant shall display a site notice by site display on or near the land on which the proposed development is to be carried out, leaving the notice in position for not less than 21 days in the period of 28 days from the date on which the local planning authority gave the notice to the applicant; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (bb (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where the site notice is, without any fault or intention of the applicant, removed, obscured or defaced before the period of 21 days referred to in sub-paragraph (aa) has elapsed, he shall be treated as having complied with the requirements of that sub-paragraph if he has taken reasonable steps for protection of the notice and, if need be, its replacement; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (v (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development shall, except to the extent that the local planning authority otherwise agree in writing, be carried out— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (aa (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where prior approval is required, in accordance with the details approved; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (bb (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where prior approval is not required, in accordance with the details submitted with the application; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (vi (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development shall be carried out— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (aa (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where approval has been given by the local planning authority, within a period of five years from the date on which approval was given; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (bb (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in any other case, within a period of five years from the date on which the local planning authority were given the information referred to in sub-paragraph (d)(ii). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The conditions in paragraph (2) do not apply to the extension or alteration of a building if the building is not on article 1(6) land except in the case of a significant extension or a significant alteration. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Development consisting of the significant extension or the significant alteration of a building may only be carried out once by virtue of Class A(a). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-9f7527639beb44a9c5103b324c8667d0][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Where development consists of works for the erection, significant extension or significant alteration of a building and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the use of the building or extension for the purposes of agriculture within the unit permanently ceases within ten years from the date on which the development was substantially completed; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] planning permission has not been granted on an application, or has not been deemed to be granted under Part III of the Act, for development for purposes other than agriculture, within three years from the date on which the use of the building or extension for the purposes of agriculture within the unit permanently ceased, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

then, unless the local planning authority have otherwise agreed in writing, the building or, in the case of development consisting of an extension, the extension, shall be removed from the land and the land shall, so far as is practicable, be restored to its condition before the development took place, or to such condition as may have been agreed in writing between the local planning authority and the developer. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(6 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Where an appeal has been made, under the Act, in relation to an application for development described in paragraph 5(b), within the period described in that paragraph, that period shall be extended until the appeal is finally determined or withdrawn. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(7 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Where development is permitted by Class A(a), the developer shall notify the local planning authority, in writing and within 7 days, of the date on which the development was substantially completed. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class B Development on units of less than 5 hectares (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**The carrying out on agricultural land comprised in an agricultural unit of not less than 0.4 but less than 5 hectares in area of development consisting of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the extension or alteration of an agricultural building; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation of additional or replacement plant or machinery; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the provision, rearrangement or replacement of a sewer, main, pipe, cable or other apparatus; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the provision, rearrangement or replacement of a private way; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the provision of a hard surface; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the deposit of waste; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (g (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the carrying out of any of the following operations in connection with fish farming, namely, repairing ponds and raceways; the installation of grading machinery, aeration equipment or flow meters and any associated channel; the dredging of ponds; and the replacement of tanks and nets, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

**where the development is reasonably necessary for the purposes of agriculture within the unit. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class B if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development would be carried out on a separate parcel of land forming part of the unit which is less than 0.4 hectare in area; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the external appearance of the premises would be materially affected; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any part of the development would be within 25 metres of a metalled part of a trunk road or classified road; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would consist of, or involve, the carrying out of any works to a building or structure used or to be used for the accommodation of livestock or the storage of slurry or sewage sludge where the building or structure is within 400 metres of the curtilage of a protected building; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c-e4e618d01e6ca1d38d93b133229812f2]... *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would relate to fish farming and would involve the placing or assembly of a tank on land or in any waters or the construction of a pond in which fish may be kept or an increase (otherwise than by the removal of silt) in the size of any tank or pond in which fish may be kept *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] ; or[^c-4e163a15fb30b2264b31fdaa2bcb01ad] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-feddc83745f793014bb658ddb470ad3f][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any building for storing fuel for or waste from a biomass boiler or an anaerobic digestion system would be used for storing waste not produced by that boiler or system or for storing fuel not produced on land within the unit. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Development is not permitted by Class B(a) if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height of any building would be increased; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the cubic content of the original building would be increased by more than 10%; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any part of any new building would be more than 30 metres from the original building; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development would involve the extension, alteration or provision of a dwelling; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any part of the development would be carried out within 5 metres of any boundary of the unit; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the ground area of any building extended by virtue of Class B(a) would exceed 465 square metres. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).3**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Development is not permitted by Class B(b) if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height of any additional plant or machinery within 3 kilometres of the perimeter of an aerodrome would exceed 3 metres; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height of any additional plant or machinery not within 3 kilometres of the perimeter of an aerodrome would exceed 12 metres; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height of any replacement plant or machinery would exceed that of the plant or machinery being replaced; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the area to be covered by the development would exceed 465 square metres calculated as described in paragraph D.2 below. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).4**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Development is not permitted by Class B(e) if the area to be covered by the development would exceed 465 square metres calculated as described in paragraph D.2 below. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).5 Conditions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development permitted by Class B and carried out within 400 metres of the curtilage of a protected building is subject to the condition that any building which is extended or altered, or any works resulting from the development, shall not be used for the accommodation of livestock except in the circumstances described in paragraph D.3 below or for the storage of slurry or sewage sludge *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] , for housing a biomass boiler or an anaerobic digestion system, for storage of fuel or waste from that boiler or system, or for housing a hydro-turbine.[^c-ca9aded77423c131b4c2aea02faf3a85] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).6**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Development consisting of the extension or alteration of a building situated on article 1(6) land or the provision, rearrangement or replacement of a private way on such land is permitted subject to— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the condition that the developer shall, before beginning the development, apply to the local planning authority for a determination as to whether the prior approval of the authority will be required to the siting, design and external appearance of the building as extended or altered or the siting and means of construction of the private way; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the conditions set out in paragraphs A.2(2)(ii) to (vi) above. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).7**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Development is permitted by Class B(f) subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] that waste materials are not brought on to the land from elsewhere for deposit unless they are for use in works described in Class B(a), (d) or (e) and are incorporated forthwith into the building or works in question; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] that the height of the surface of the land will not be materially increased by the deposit. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**B.8 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-923ced6e9054db10796427d18f0d5bb5][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Development is permitted by Class B(a) subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Where development consists of works for the significant extension or significant alteration of a building and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the use of the building or extension for the purposes of agriculture within the unit permanently ceases within ten years from the date on which the development was substantially completed; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] planning permission has not been granted on an application, or has not been deemed to be granted under Part III of the Act, for development for purposes other than agriculture, within three years from the date on which the use of the building or extension for the purposes of agriculture within the unit permanently ceased, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  then, unless the local planning authority have otherwise agreed in writing, the extension, in the case of development consisting of an extension, shall be removed from the land and the land shall, so far as is practicable, be restored to its condition before the development took place, or to such condition as may have been agreed in writing between the local planning authority and the developer. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Where an appeal has been made, under the Act, in relation to an application for development described in paragraph B.8(a)(ii), within the period described in that paragraph, that period shall be extended until the appeal is finally determined or withdrawn. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The developer shall notify the local planning authority in writing and within 7 days, of the date on which the development was substantially completed. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class C Mineral working for agricultural purposes (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### C (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**The winning and working on land held or occupied with land used for the purposes of agriculture of any minerals reasonably necessary for agricultural purposes within the agricultural unit of which it forms part. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### C (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class C if any excavation would be made within 25 metres of a metalled part of a trunk road or classified road. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### C (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Condition (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class C subject to the condition that no mineral extracted during the course of the operation shall be moved to any place outside the land from which it was extracted, except to land which is held or occupied with that land and is used for the purposes of agriculture. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### D (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Interpretation of Part 6 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Part 6— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “agricultural land" means land which, before development permitted by this Part is carried out, is land in use for agriculture and which is so used for the purposes of a trade or business, and excludes any dwellinghouse or garden; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “agricultural unit" means agricultural land which is occupied as a unit for the purposes of agriculture, including— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (a) any dwelling or other building on that land occupied for the purpose of farming the land by the person who occupies the unit, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (b) any dwelling on that land occupied by a farmworker; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “building" does not include anything resulting from engineering operations; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “fish farming" means the breeding, rearing or keeping of fish or shellfish (which includes any kind of crustacean and mollusc); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “livestock" includes fish or shellfish which are farmed; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “protected building" means any permanent building which is normally occupied by people or would be so occupied, if it were in use for purposes for which it is apt; but does not include— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i) a building within the agricultural unit; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii) a dwelling or other building on another agricultural unit which is used for or in connection with agriculture; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “significant extension" and “significant alteration" mean any extension or alteration of the building where the cubic content of the original building would be exceeded by more than 10% or the height of the building as extended or altered would exceed the height of the original building; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “slurry" means animal faeces and urine (whether or not water has been added for handling); and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “tank" includes any cage and any other structure for use in fish farming. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**D (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] For the purposes of Part 6— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] an area calculated as described in this paragraph comprises the ground area which would be covered by the proposed development, together with the ground area of any building (other than a dwelling), or any structure, works, plant, machinery, ponds or tanks within the same unit which are being provided or have been provided within the preceding two years and any part of which would be within 90 metres of the proposed development; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] 400 metres is to be measured along the ground. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**D (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).3**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The circumstances referred to in paragraphs A.2(1)(a) and B.5 are— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] that no other suitable building or structure, 400 metres or more from the curtilage of a protected building, is available to accommodate the livestock; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] that the need to accommodate the livestock arises from— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (aa (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] quarantine requirements; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (bb (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] an emergency due to another building or structure in which the livestock could otherwise be accommodated being unavailable because it has been damaged or destroyed by fire, flood or storm; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of animals normally kept out of doors, they require temporary accommodation in a building or other structure— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (aa (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] because they are sick or giving birth or newly born; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (bb (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] to provide shelter against extreme weather conditions. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**D (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).4**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] For the purposes of paragraph A.2(2)(c), the relevant area is the area of the proposed excavation or the area on which it is proposed to deposit waste together with the aggregate of the areas of all other excavations within the unit which have not been filled and of all other parts of the unit on or under which waste has been deposited and has not been removed. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**D (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).5**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] In paragraph A.2(2)(iv), “site notice" means a notice containing— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the name of the applicant, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the address or location of the proposed development, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a description of the proposed development and of the materials to be used, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a statement that the prior approval of the authority will be required to the siting, design and external appearance of the building, the siting and means of construction of the private way, the siting of the excavation or deposit or the siting and appearance of the tank, as the case may be, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the name and address of the local planning authority, and which is signed and dated by or on behalf of the applicant. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**D (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).6**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] For the purposes of Class B— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the erection of any additional building within the curtilage of another building is to be treated as the extension of that building and the additional building is not to be treated as an original building; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where two or more original buildings are within the same curtilage and are used for the same undertaking they are to be treated as a single original building in making any measurement in connection with the extension or alteration of either of them. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**D (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).7**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] In Class C, “the purposes of agriculture" includes fertilising land used for the purposes of agriculture and the maintenance, improvement or alteration of any buildings, structures or works occupied or used for such purposes on land so used. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**D.8 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-cd5f4efabd18cb442745a750e73ca036][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] In Class A(a), “reasonably necessary for the purposes of agriculture” includes, in relation to the erection, extension or alteration of a building, for housing a biomass boiler or an anaerobic digestion system; for storage of fuel for or waste from that boiler or system; or for housing a hydro-turbine. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**D.9 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] In Class B(a), “reasonably necessary for the purposes of agriculture” includes, in relation to the extension or alteration of an agricultural building, for housing a biomass boiler or an anaerobic digestion system; for storage of fuel for or waste from that boiler or system; or for housing a hydro-turbine. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

### PART 6A — Community Growing Spaces[^c-be5fe1e1485ecb5caa48aaa0dad73eca]

#### Class A

##### A. Permitted development

**Development consisting of the erection, extension, alteration or replacement of storage sheds and greenhouses on community growing spaces.**

##### A.1. Development not permitted

Development is not permitted by Class A if—

- (a) the development would be carried out on a community growing space of less than 62 square metres in area;
- (b) on a community growing space of less than 125 square metres in area, it would result in the presence of more than either—
  - (i) one storage shed; or
  - (ii) one greenhouse;
- (c) on a community growing space of 125 square metres or more in area, it would result in the presence of more than one storage shed and one greenhouse;
- (d) the ground area of the storage shed or greenhouse would exceed 6 square metres when measured externally;
- (e) any part of the storage shed or greenhouse would exceed 2.2 metres in height;
- (f) the development would be on article 1(5) land;
- (g) the development would be on land within a World Heritage Site;
- (h) the development would be within the curtilage of a listed building; or
- (i) the development would be within—
  - (i) 8 metres of a non-tidal main river (or within 8 metres of any flood defence structure or culvert on that river); or
  - (ii) within 16 metres of a tidal main river (or within 16 metres of any flood defence structure or culvert on that river).

##### A.2. Interpretation of Class A

For the purposes of Class A—

- “community growing space” means—
  - (a) an allotment including an allotment garden within the meaning of the Allotments Act 1922; or
  - (b) any other land used or intended for use—
    - (i) by one or more communities,
    - (ii) wholly or mainly for the cultivation of vegetables, fruit, herbs or flowers, and
    - (iii) otherwise than with a view to making a profit;
- “culvert” means a covered channel or pipe which prevents the obstruction of a main river or drainage path by an artificial construction;
- “flood defence structure” means any permanent works constructed, operated or maintained by the Natural Resources Body for Wales, for the purposes of managing flood risk;
- “greenhouse” means a structure such as a glasshouse or polytunnel in which vegetables, fruit, herbs or flowers are cultivated under cover in an enclosed space, but does not include a structure in which livestock are kept;
- “main river” has the meaning given in section 113(1) of the Water Resources Act 1991;
- “non-tidal main river” means any part of a main river that is not a tidal main river;
- “normal tidal limit” means the normal tidal limit as marked on the Ordnance Maps (1:25,000 scale);
- “tidal main river” means that part of a main river downstream of the normal tidal limit.
