##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class A if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would consist of or include the provision or alteration of a dwelling; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height of any building or works within 3 kilometres of the perimeter of an aerodrome would exceed 3 metres in height; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c-95bc0c6cc4e2ed80398bd99be7f19a8a]... *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any part of the development would be within 25 metres of the metalled portion of a trunk road or classified road *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] ; or[^c-90bc23e6d9cae78f799ab3bd2dfb20cc] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-1d7afe08c79017533bc8f7e3ea8f7219][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any building for storing fuel for or waste from a biomass boiler or an anaerobic digestion system would be used for storing fuel not produced on land which is occupied together with that building for the purposes of forestry or waste not produced by that boiler or system. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] (1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Subject to paragraph (3), development consisting of the erection of a building or the extension or alteration of a building or the formation or alteration of a private way is permitted by Class A subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the developer shall, before beginning the development, apply to the local planning authority for a determination as to whether the prior approval of the authority will be required to the siting, design and external appearance of the building or, as the case may be, the siting and means of construction of the private way; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the application shall be accompanied by a written description of the proposed development, the materials to be used and a plan indicating the site together with any fee required to be paid; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development shall not be begun before the occurrence of one of the following— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the receipt by the applicant from the local planning authority of a written notice of their determination that such prior approval is not required; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where the local planning authority give the applicant notice within 28 days following the date of receiving his application of their determination that such prior approval is required, the giving of such approval; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the expiry of 28 days following the date on which the application was received by the local planning authority without the local planning authority making any determination as to whether such approval is required or notifying the applicant of their determination; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where the local planning authority give the applicant notice that such prior approval is required the applicant shall display a site notice by site display on or near the land on which the proposed development is to be carried out, leaving the notice in position for not less than 21 days in the period of 28 days from the date on which the local planning authority gave the notice to the applicant; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where the site notice is, without any fault or intention of the applicant, removed, obscured or defaced before the period of 21 days referred to in sub-paragraph (i) has elapsed, he shall be treated as having complied with the requirements of that sub-paragraph if he has taken reasonable steps for protection of the notice and, if need be, its replacement; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development shall, except to the extent that the local planning authority otherwise agree in writing, be carried out— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where prior approval is required, in accordance with the details approved; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where prior approval is not required, in accordance with the details submitted with the application; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development shall be carried out— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where approval has been given by the local planning authority, within a period of five years from the date on which approval was given, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in any other case, within a period of five years from the date on which the local planning authority were given the information referred to in sub-paragraph (b). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] In the case of development consisting of the significant extension or the significant alteration of the building such development may be carried out only once. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Paragraph (1) does not preclude the extension or alteration of a building if the building is not on article 1(6) land except in the case of a significant extension or a significant alteration. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).3 Interpretation of Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Class A— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “significant extension" and “significant alteration" mean any extension or alteration of the building where the cubic content of the original building would be exceeded by more than 10% or the height of the building as extended or altered would exceed the height of the original building; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “site notice" means a notice containing— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (a) the name of the applicant, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (b) the address or location of the proposed development, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (c) a description of the proposed development and of the materials to be used, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (d) a statement that the prior approval of the authority will be required to the siting, design and external appearance of the building or, as the case may be, the siting and means of construction of the private way, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (e) the name and address of the local planning authority, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  and which is signed and dated by or on behalf of the applicant. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A.4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-103a9386a35c15fbd7974aca1f0e0149][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Class A(a) “the purposes of forestry” includes works for the erection, extension or alteration of a building for housing a biomass boiler or an anaerobic digestion system, for storage of fuel for or waste from that boiler or system, or for housing a hydro-turbine. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A.1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class A if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] if within 10 metres of a boundary of the curtilage of the premises, 5 metres; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in all other cases, the height of the highest building within the curtilage of the premises or 15 metres, whichever is lower; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height of the building as extended or altered would exceed— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] if within 10 metres of a boundary of the curtilage of the premises, 5 metres; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in all other cases, the height of the building being extended or altered; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any part of the development, would be within 5 metres of any boundary of the curtilage of the premises; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the gross floor space of any new building erected would exceed 100 square metres; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the gross floor space of the original building would be exceeded by more than— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] 10% in respect of development on any article 1(5) land or within a World Heritage Site, or 25% in any other case; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] 500 square metres in respect of development on any article 1(5) land or within a World Heritage Site, or 1,000 square metres in any other case; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  whichever is the lesser; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development would lead to a reduction in the space available for the parking or turning of vehicles; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (g (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development would be within the curtilage of a listed building. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A.2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Conditions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class A subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development must be within the curtilage of an existing industrial building or warehouse; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any building as erected, extended or altered may only be used— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of an industrial building, for the carrying out of an industrial process for the purposes of the undertaking, for research and development of products or processes, or the provision of employee facilities ancillary to the undertaking; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of a warehouse, for storage or distribution for the purposes of the undertaking or the provision of employee facilities ancillary to the undertaking; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] no building as erected, extended or altered may be used to provide employee facilities— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] between 7.00 pm and 6.30 am, for employees other than those present at the premises of the undertaking for the purpose of their employment, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] at all, if a notifiable quantity of a hazardous substance is present at the premises of the undertaking; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any new building erected must, in the case of article 1(5) land or land within a World Heritage Site, be constructed using materials which have a similar external appearance to those used for the existing industrial building or warehouse; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any extension or alteration must, in the case of article 1(5) land or land within a World Heritage Site, be constructed using materials which have a similar external appearance to those used for the building being extended or altered. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A.3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation of Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Class A— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where two or more original buildings are within the same curtilage and are used for the same undertaking, they are to be treated as a single original building in making any measurement; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] “original building” does not include any building erected at any time under Class A; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] “employee facilities” means social, care or recreational facilities provided for employees of the undertaking, including crèche facilities provided for the children of such employees. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**Development carried out on industrial land for the purposes of an industrial process consisting of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation of additional or replacement plant or machinery, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the provision, rearrangement or replacement of a sewer, main, pipe, cable or other apparatus, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the provision, rearrangement or replacement of a private way, private railway, siding or conveyor. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### B.1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development described in Class B(a) is not permitted if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would materially affect the external appearance of the premises of the undertaking concerned; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any plant or machinery would exceed a height of 15 metres above ground level or the height of anything replaced, whichever is the greater. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

In Class B, “industrial land” means land used for the carrying out of an industrial process, including land used for the purposes of an industrial undertaking as a dock, harbour or quay but does not include land in or adjacent to and occupied together with a mine. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**Development consisting of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the provision of a hard surface within the curtilage of an industrial building or warehouse to be used for the purpose of the undertaking concerned; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the replacement in whole or in part of such a surface. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### C.1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class C if the development would be within the curtilage of a listed building. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### C.2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Conditions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] porous or permeable; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] provided to direct run-off water from the hard surface to a porous or permeable area or surface within the curtilage of the industrial building or warehouse; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] permanently maintained so that it continues to comply with the requirements of paragraph (a) and (b). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**The erection or construction of a refuse or cycle store within the curtilage of an industrial building or warehouse. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### E.1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class E if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the gross floor space of the building or enclosure would exceed 20 square metres; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any part of the building or enclosure erected would be within; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] 5 metres of any boundary of the curtilage of the premises; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] 20 metres of any building used for residential purposes; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height of the building or enclosure would exceed 2.5 metres; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development would be on article 1(5) land; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development would be on land within a World Heritage Site; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development would be within the curtilage of a listed building; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (g (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development would lead to a reduction in the space available for the parking and turning of vehicles. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class E subject to the condition that the building or enclosure is only used for the storage of refuse or bicycles. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### E.3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation of Class E (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Class E— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “cycle store” means a building or enclosure designed to be used for the storage of bicycles; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “refuse store” means a building or enclosure designed to be used for the storage of refuse which may include recycling; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “residential purposes” means a building used for any purpose within Part C of the Schedule to the Use Classes Order, as a hostel or as a flat and includes buildings used in part for residential purposes and in part for other purposes. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### F (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation of Part 8 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Part 8— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “industrial building” means a building used for the carrying out of an industrial process and includes a building used for the carrying out of such a process on land used as a dock, harbour or quay for the purposes of an industrial undertaking and land used for research and development of products or processes, but does not include a building on land in or adjacent to and occupied together with a mine; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “warehouse” means a building used for any purpose within Class B8 (storage or distribution) of the Schedule to the Use Classes Order but does not include a building on land in or adjacent to and occupied together with a mine. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**The erection or construction and the maintenance, improvement or other alteration by a local authority or by an urban development corporation of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **any small ancillary building, works or equipment on land belonging to or maintained by them required for the purposes of any function exercised by them on that land otherwise than as statutory undertakers; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **lamp standards, information kiosks, passenger shelters, public shelters and seats, telephone boxes, fire alarms, public drinking fountains, horse troughs, refuse bins or baskets, barriers for the control of people waiting to enter public service vehicles, and similar structures or works required in connection with the operation of any public service administered by them. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-b5e4d7e5b23e35a199c376cc709487f0][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **electric vehicle charging points and any associated infrastructure. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Interpretation of Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Class A— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

“urban development corporation" has the same meaning as in Part XVI of the Local Government, Planning and Land Act 1980 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c17825551] (urban development). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The reference in Class A to any small ancillary building, works or equipment is a reference to any ancillary building, works or equipment not exceeding 4 metres in height or 200 cubic metres in capacity. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

### PART 12A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — Emergency Development by Local Authorities (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-5258455d94cd4c978ed6a1e411c0e41c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**Development by a local authority on land owned, leased, occupied or maintained by it for the purposes of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **preventing an emergency; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **reducing, controlling or mitigating the effects of an emergency; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **taking other action in connection with an emergency. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### A.1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Conditions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class A subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] if the developer is not also the local planning authority, the developer must, as soon as reasonably practicable notify the local planning authority of that development; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c-e3b1b9108461a2ea08be9b3035d3c47d]... *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-a3b6210df566a71dccfce3582801de64] on or before the expiry date— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any use of that land for a purpose of Class A must cease and any buildings, plant, machinery, structures and erections permitted by Class A must be removed; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the land must be restored to its condition before the development took place, or to such other state as may be agreed in writing between the local planning authority and the developer; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-35eea346e0584a21c8db9a055adb4b21][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in sub-paragraph (b), “the expiry date” means— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] for developments which begin before 30 March 2021 the date eighteen months after the date on which the development began; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] for developments which begin on or after 30 March 2021 the date twelve months after the date on which the development began. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A.2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation of Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] For the purposes of Class A, “emergency” means an event or situation which threatens serious damage to human welfare in a place in the United Kingdom. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] For the purposes of paragraph (1), an event or situation threatens damage to human welfare only if it involves, causes or may cause— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] loss of human life; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] human illness or injury; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] homelessness; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] damage to property; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] disruption of a supply of money, food, water, energy or fuel; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] disruption of a system of communication; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (g (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] disruption of facilities for transport; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (h (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] disruption of services relating to health. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

### PART 15 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — Development by the Environment Agency and the Natural Resources Body for Wales (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-d0fd104f9016a07096e05eb928b34c06][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**Development *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] by the Environment Agency or the Natural Resources Body for Wales for the purposes of their respective functions[^c-6991a82bf9ad4a5375c2927c916df66d] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c], consisting of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **development not above ground level required in connection with conserving, redistributing or augmenting water resources, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **development in, on or under any watercourse or land drainage works and required in connection with the improvement, maintenance or repair of that watercourse or those works, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the provision of a building, plant, machinery or apparatus in, on, over or under land for the purpose of survey or investigation, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the maintenance, improvement or repair of works for measuring the flow in any watercourse or channel, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **any works authorised by or required in connection with an order made under section 73 of the Water Resources Act 1991 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c17825571] (power to make ordinary and emergency drought orders), *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **any other development in, on, over or under their operational land, other than the provision of a building but including the extension or alteration of a building. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class A if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class A(a) development, it would include the construction of a reservoir, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class A(f) development, it would consist of or include the extension or alteration of a building so that— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] its design or external appearance would be materially affected, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height of the original building would be exceeded, or the cubic content of the original building would be exceeded by more than 25%, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the floor space of the original building would be exceeded by more than 1,000 square metres, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class A(f) development, it would consist of the installation or erection of any plant or machinery exceeding 15 metres in height or the height of anything it replaces, whichever is the greater. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Condition (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class A(c) subject to the condition that, on completion of the survey or investigation, or at the expiration of six months from the commencement of the development concerned, whichever is the sooner, all such operations shall cease and all such buildings, plant, machinery and apparatus shall be removed and the land restored as soon as reasonably practicable to its former condition (or to any other condition which may be agreed with the local planning authority). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**Development by or on behalf of a sewerage undertaker consisting of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **development not above ground level required in connection with the provision, improvement, maintenance or repair of a sewer, outfall pipe, sludge main or associated apparatus; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the provision of a building, plant, machinery or apparatus in, on, over or under land for the purpose of survey or investigation; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the maintenance, improvement or repair of works for measuring the flow in any watercourse or channel; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **any works authorised by or required in connection with an order made under section 73 of the Water Resources Act 1991 (power to make ordinary and emergency drought orders); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **any other development in, on, over or under their operational land, other than the provision of a building but including the extension or alteration of a building. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class A(e) if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would consist of or include the extension or alteration of a building so that— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] its design or external appearance would be materially affected; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height of the original building would be exceeded, or the cubic content of the original building would be exceeded, by more than 25%; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the floor space of the original building would be exceeded by more than 1,000 square metres; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would consist of the installation or erection of any plant or machinery exceeding 15 metres in height or the height of anything it replaces, whichever is the greater. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Condition (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class A(b) subject to the condition that, on completion of the survey or investigation, or at the expiration of 6 months from the commencement of the development concerned, whichever is the sooner, all such operations shall cease and all such buildings, plant, machinery and apparatus shall be removed and the land restored as soon as reasonably practicable to its former condition (or to any other condition which may be agreed with the local planning authority). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).3 Interpretation of Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Class A— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “associated apparatus", in relation to any sewer, main or pipe, means pumps, machinery or apparatus associated with the relevant sewer, main or pipe; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “sludge main" means a pipe or system of pipes (together with any pumps or other machinery or apparatus associated with it) for the conveyance of the residue of water or sewage treated in a water or sewage treatment works as the case may be, including final effluent or the products of the dewatering or incineration of such residue, or partly for any of those purposes and partly for the conveyance of trade effluent or its residue. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

### PART 17 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — DEVELOPMENT BY STATUTORY UNDERTAKERS (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class A Railway or light railway undertakings (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**Development by railway undertakers on their operational land, required in connection with the movement of traffic by rail. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class A if it consists of or includes— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the construction of a railway, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the construction or erection of a hotel, railway station or bridge, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the construction or erection otherwise than wholly within a railway station of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] an office, residential or educational building, or a building used for an industrial process, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a car park, shop, restaurant, garage, petrol filling station or other building or structure provided under transport legislation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Interpretation of Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Class A, references to the construction or erection of any building or structure include references to the reconstruction or alteration of a building or structure where its design or external appearance would be materially affected. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class B Dock, pier, harbour, water transport, canal or inland navigation undertakings (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**Development on operational land by statutory undertakers or their lessees in respect of dock, pier, harbour, water transport, or canal or inland navigation undertakings, required— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **for the purposes of shipping, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **in connection with the embarking, disembarking, loading, discharging or transport of passengers, livestock or goods at a dock, pier or harbour, or with the movement of traffic by canal or inland navigation or by any railway forming part of the undertaking. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class B if it consists of or includes— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the construction or erection of a hotel, or of a bridge or other building not required in connection with the handling of traffic, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the construction or erection otherwise than wholly within the limits of a dock, pier or harbour of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] an educational building, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a car park, shop, restaurant, garage, petrol filling station or other building provided under transport legislation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Interpretation of Class B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Class B, references to the construction or erection of any building or structure include references to the reconstruction or alteration of a building or structure where its design or external appearance would be materially affected, and the reference to operational land includes land designated by an order made under section 14 or 16 of the Harbours Act 1964 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c17825581] (orders for securing harbour efficiency etc., and orders conferring powers for improvement, construction etc. of harbours), and which has come into force, whether or not the order was subject to the provisions of the Statutory Orders (Special Procedure) Act 1945 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c17825591]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**The improvement, maintenance or repair of an inland waterway (other than a commercial waterway or cruising waterway) to which section 104 of the Transport Act 1968 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c17825601] (classification of the Board’s waterways) applies, and the repair or maintenance of a culvert, weir, lock, aqueduct, sluice, reservoir, let-off valve or other work used in connection with the control and operation of such a waterway. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
