**Development for the purposes of their undertaking by statutory undertakers for the supply of water or hydraulic power consisting of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **development not above ground level required in connection with the supply of water or for conserving, redistributing or augmenting water resources, or for the conveyance of water treatment sludge, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **development in, on or under any watercourse and required in connection with the improvement or maintenance of that watercourse, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the provision of a building, plant, machinery or apparatus in, on, over or under land for the purpose of survey or investigation, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the maintenance, improvement or repair of works for measuring the flow in any watercourse or channel, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation in a water distribution system of a booster station, valve house, meter or switch-gear house, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **any works authorised by or required in connection with an order made under section 73 of the Water Resources Act 1991 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c17825611] (power to make ordinary and emergency drought orders), *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (g (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **any other development in, on, over or under operational land other than the provision of a building but including the extension or alteration of a building. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### E (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class E if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class E(a) development, it would include the construction of a reservoir, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class E(e) development involving the installation of a station or house exceeding 29 cubic metres in capacity, that installation is carried out at or above ground level or under a highway used by vehicular traffic, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class E(g) development, it would consist of or include the extension or alteration of a building so that— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] its design or external appearance would be materially affected; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height of the original building would be exceeded, or the cubic content of the original building would be exceeded by more than 25%, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the floor space of the original building would be exceeded by more than 1,000 square metres, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class E(g) development, it would consist of the installation or erection of any plant or machinery exceeding 15 metres in height or the height of anything it replaces, whichever is the greater. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### E (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Condition (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class E(c) subject to the condition that, on completion of the survey or investigation, or at the expiration of six months from the commencement of the development, whichever is the sooner, all such operations shall cease and all such buildings, plant, machinery and apparatus shall be removed and the land restored as soon as reasonably practicable to its former condition (or to any other condition which may be agreed with the local planning authority). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class F (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) Public gas transporters (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-32fd7dd83b03f250b5ad5cc9b3c7c4ea][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### F (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**Development by a *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] public gas transporter *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] required for the purposes of its undertaking consisting of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the laying underground of mains, pipes or other apparatus; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation in a gas distribution system of apparatus for measuring, recording, controlling or varying the pressure, flow or volume of gas, and structures for housing such apparatus; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the construction in any storage area or protective area specified in an order made under section 4 of the Gas Act 1965 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c17825621] (storage authorisation orders), of boreholes, and the erection or construction in any such area of any plant or machinery required in connection with the construction of such boreholes; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the placing and storage on land of pipes and other apparatus to be included in a main or pipe which is being or is about to be laid or constructed in pursuance of planning permission granted or deemed to be granted under Part III of the Act (control over development); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the erection on operational land of the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] public gas transporter *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] of a building solely for the protection of plant or machinery; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **any other development carried out in, on, over or under the operational land of the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] public gas transporter *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### F (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class F if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class F(b) development involving the installation of a structure for housing apparatus exceeding 29 cubic metres in capacity, that installation would be carried out at or above ground level, or under a highway used by vehicular traffic, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class F(c) development— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the borehole is shown in an order approved by the Secretary of State for Trade and Industry for the purpose of section 4(6) of the Gas Act 1965; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any plant or machinery would exceed 6 metres in height, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class F(e) development, the building would exceed 15 metres in height, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class F(f) development— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would consist of or include the erection of a building, or the reconstruction or alteration of a building where its design or external appearance would be materially affected; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would involve the installation of plant or machinery exceeding 15 metres in height, or capable without the carrying out of additional works of being extended to a height exceeding 15 metres; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would consist of or include the replacement of any plant or machinery, by plant or machinery exceeding 15 metres in height or exceeding the height of the plant or machinery replaced, whichever is the greater. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### F (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Conditions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class F subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class F(a) development, not less than eight weeks before the beginning of operations to lay a notifiable pipe-line, the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] public gas transporter *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] shall give notice in writing to the local planning authority of its intention to carry out that development, identifying the land under which the pipe-line is to be laid, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class F(d) development, on completion of the laying or construction of the main or pipe, or at the expiry of a period of nine months from the beginning of the development, whichever is the sooner, any pipes or other apparatus still stored on the land shall be removed and the land restored as soon as reasonably practicable to its condition before the development took place (or to any other condition which may be agreed with the local planning authority), *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class F(e) development, approval of the details of the design and external appearance of the building shall be obtained, before the development is begun, from— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in Greater London or a metropolitan county, the local planning authority, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in a National Park *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in England[^c-697426b63419532570e296a7790cb2bc] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c], outside a metropolitan county, the county planning authority, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in any other case *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in England[^c-d3ac354fb80263fbd807f43058cf8f14] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c], the district planning authority *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c17825631]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iv (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-794bf8257a0bf6bb27b9d09c26d82c0e][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in Wales, the local planning authority. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class G Electricity undertakings (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### G (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**Development by statutory undertakers for the generation, transmission or supply of electricity for the purposes of their undertaking consisting of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation or replacement in, on, over or under land of an electric line and the construction of shafts and tunnels and the installation or replacement of feeder or service pillars or transforming or switching stations or chambers reasonably necessary in connection with an electric line; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation or replacement of any *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] electronic communications line[^c-fc781da5c228472fb530f765be2911c8] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] which connects any part of an electric line to any electrical plant or building, and the installation or replacement of any support for any such line; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the sinking of boreholes to ascertain the nature of the subsoil and the installation of any plant or machinery reasonably necessary in connection with such boreholes; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the extension or alteration of buildings on operational land; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the erection on operational land of the undertaking or a building solely for the protection of plant or machinery; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **any other development carried out in, on, over or under the operational land of the undertaking. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### G (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class G if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class G(a) development— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ai (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-b50e44d9f0c1a49aa9f67649ede71c05][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would consist of or include the installation or replacement of a devolved associated line within the meaning set out in paragraph A.3(1) of Part 17A; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would consist of or include the installation or replacement of an electric line to which section 37(1) of the Electricity Act 1989 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c17825641] (consent required for overhead lines) applies; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would consist of or include the installation or replacement at or above ground level or under a highway used by vehicular traffic, of a chamber for housing apparatus and the chamber would exceed 29 cubic metres in capacity; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class G(b) development— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development would take place in a National Park, an area of outstanding natural beauty, or a site of special scientific interest; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height of any support would exceed 15 metres; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] electronic communications line *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] would exceed 1,000 metres in length; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class G(d) development— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height of the original building would be exceeded; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the cubic content of the original building would be exceeded by more than 25% or, in the case of any building on article 1(5) land, by more than 10%, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the floor space of the original building would be exceeded by more than 1,000 square metres or, in the case of any building on article 1(5) land, by more than 500 square metres; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class G(e) development, the building would exceed 15 metres in height, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class G(f) development, it would consist of or include— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the erection of a building, or the reconstruction or alteration of a building where its design or external appearance would be materially affected, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the installation or erection by way of addition or replacement of any plant or machinery exceeding 15 metres in height or the height of any plant or machinery replaced, whichever is the greater. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### G (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Conditions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class G subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class G(a) development consisting of or including the replacement of an existing electric line, compliance with any conditions contained in a planning permission relating to the height, design or position of the existing electric line which are capable of being applied to the replacement line; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class G(a) development consisting of or including the installation of a temporary electric line providing a diversion for an existing electric line, on the ending of the diversion or at the end of a period of six months from the completion of the installation (whichever is the sooner) the temporary electric line shall be removed and the land on which any operations have been carried out to install that line shall be restored as soon as reasonably practicable to its condition before the installation took place; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class G(c) development, on the completion of that development, or at the end of a period of six months from the beginning of that development (whichever is the sooner) any plant or machinery installed shall be removed and the land shall be restored as soon as reasonably practicable to its condition before the development took place; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class G(e) development, approval of details of the design and external appearance of the buildings shall be obtained, before development is begun, from— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in Greater London or a metropolitan county, the local planning authority, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in a National Park *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in England[^c-9769b40ec66c7cc39d116b52e0112477] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c], outside a metropolitan county, the county planning authority, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in any other case *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in England[^c-289ff6d9d066085e2ab293981db7b723] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c], the district planning authority *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c17825651]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iv (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ab6d59df432300e73820a253231242f3][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in Wales, the local planning authority. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### G (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).3 Interpretation of Class G (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Class G(a), “electric line" has the meaning assigned to that term by section 64(1) of the Electricity Act 1989 (interpretation etc. of Part 1). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**G (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).4**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] For the purposes of Class G(b), “electrical plant" has the meaning assigned to that term by the said section 64(1) and “ *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]electronic communications line *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]" means a wire or cable (including its casing or coating) which forms part of *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] an electronic communications apparatus *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] within the meaning assigned to that term by *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] paragraph 5 of the electronic communications code set out in Schedule 3A to the Communications Act 2003[^c-8956e1568fa54ec13029b166f0795ea4] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] (the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] electronic communications code *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**G (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).5**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] For the purposes of Class G(d), (e) and (f), the land of the holder of a licence under section 6(2) of the Electricity Act 1989 (licences authorising supply etc.) shall be treated as operational land if it would be operational land within section 263 of the Act *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c17825671] (meaning of “operational land") if such licence holders were statutory undertakers for the purpose of that section. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class H Tramway or road transport undertakings (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### H (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**Development required for the purposes of the carrying on of any tramway or road transport undertaking consisting of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation of posts, overhead wires, underground cables, feeder pillars or transformer boxes in, on, over or adjacent to a highway for the purpose of supplying current to public service vehicles; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation of tramway tracks, and conduits, drains and pipes in connection with such tracks for the working of tramways; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation of telephone cables and apparatus, huts, stop posts and signs required in connection with the operation of public service vehicles; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the erection or construction and the maintenance, improvement or other alteration of passenger shelters and barriers for the control of people waiting to enter public service vehicles; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **any other development on operational land of the undertaking. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### H (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class H if it would consist of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class H(a) development, the installation of a structure exceeding 17 cubic metres in capacity, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class H(e) development— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the erection of a building or the reconstruction or alteration of a building where its design or external appearance would be materially affected, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the installation or erection by way of addition or replacement of any plant or machinery which would exceed 15 metres in height or the height of any plant or machinery it replaces, whichever is the greater, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] development, not wholly within a bus or tramway station, in pursuance of powers contained in transport legislation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class I Lighthouse undertakings (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### I (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**Development required for the purposes of the functions of a general or local lighthouse authority under the Merchant Shipping Act 1894 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c17825681] and any other statutory provision made with respect to a local lighthouse authority, or in the exercise by a local lighthouse authority of rights, powers or duties acquired by usage prior to the 1894 Act. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### I (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class I if it consists of or includes the erection of offices, or the reconstruction or alteration of offices where their design or external appearance would be materially affected. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class J (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) Universal Services Providers (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-28d35b5c029e86d38e1b36e25aae1c3a][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### J (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**Development required for the purposes of *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a universal service provider (within the meaning of[^c-d1f9019531e155f88cc6402bd68a264b] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Part 3 of the Postal Services Act 2011[^c-e34e184139e66b2fcbb9aa514aa7cd56] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]) in connection with the provision of a universal postal service (within the meaning of *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] that Part[^c-290d0106516d5c360bedf54e98e20959] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]) *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] consisting of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation of posting boxes or self-service machines, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **any other development carried out in, on, over or under the operational land of the undertaking. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### J (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class J if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would consist of or include the erection of a building, or the reconstruction or alteration of a building where its design or external appearance would be materially affected, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would consist of or include the installation or erection by way of addition or replacement of any plant or machinery which would exceed 15 metres in height or the height of any existing plant or machinery, whichever is the greater. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### K (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation of Part 17 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Part 17— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

“transport legislation" means section 14(1)(d) of the Transport Act 1962 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c17825691] (supplemental provisions relating to the Boards’ powers) or section 10(1)(x) of the Transport Act 1968 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c17825701] (general powers of Passenger Transport Executive). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

### PART 17A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — Installation of devolved associated lines (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-5a5a4958aed412176694ad348722efd8][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted Development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**Development by statutory undertakers for the generation, transmission or supply of electricity for the purposes of their statutory undertaking consisting of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation of a devolved associated line with a nominal voltage not exceeding 20 kilovolts used or intended to be used for supplying a single consumer; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation of so much of a devolved associated line as is or will be within premises in the occupation or control of the person responsible for its installation; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation of a devolved associated line which— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **connects an electric line installed below ground with apparatus mounted on a pole or structure; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **is attached to the pole or structure throughout its length except where it passes through a fuse or other apparatus; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation for a period not exceeding six months of a devolved associated line which connects two points on an existing line which are no further apart than the maximum distance so as to provide a diversion for the existing line; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation of a devolved associated line attached to a building where the building in question crosses a road, railway or watercourse and its principal purpose is not the support of the line; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation of a devolved associated line which replaces an existing line whether or not it is installed in the same position as the existing line in question; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (g (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation of one or more additional poles to support an existing devolved associated line; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (h (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the installation of a devolved associated line which has been, or is to be, installed in accordance with a power conferred by, or by an order made under, an Act of Parliament or an Act of the National Assembly for Wales. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### A.1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Development is not permitted by Class A if: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class A(d) and (f) development, any part of the line is within a European site or a site of special scientific interest; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] (save as provided for in paragraph A.2(3)) in the case of any Class A(d), (f) or (g) development— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the line is to be installed in a different position from the existing line; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height above the surface of the ground of any support for the line will exceed the height of the highest support which is to be replaced; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the installation will be in a National Park or an area of outstanding natural beauty, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  and it is determined there is likely to be a significant adverse effect on the environment; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class A(e) development, the building in question is a scheduled monument, a listed building or in a conservation area; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of any Class A(f) development the line has a nominal voltage greater than the nominal voltage of the existing line. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] For the purposes of paragraph A.1(b) it is determined that there is likely to be a significant adverse effect on the environment if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] notice is given by the person proposing to carry out the installation to the local planning authority of that proposal; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the authority, within six weeks of receiving that notice— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] determines that if the installation were completed in accordance with the proposal it would in the opinion of that authority be likely to have a significant adverse effect on the environment; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] notifies the person by whom the notice was given and the Welsh Ministers of that determination. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A.2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Conditions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Development is permitted in the case of any Class A (f) and (g) development subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] that any conditions applicable to the existing line contained in— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a consent granted under section 37(1) of the Electricity Act 1989 (consent required for overhead lines) or paragraph 10(b) of the Schedule to the Electric Lighting (Clauses) Act 1899; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] an order granting development consent under the Planning Act 2008, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a planning permission relating to the height, design or position of the existing line which are capable of being applied to the installation; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] or[^c-c8308352abe9aba14d57f746bfdbae61] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iv (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] an order granting infrastructure consent under the Infrastructure (Wales) Act 2024; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  are complied with; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] that the height above the surface of the ground of any support for the line does not exceed the height of the highest existing support or support which is being replaced by more than 10 per cent; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] that where the line is installed in a different position from the existing line the distance between any small support and the existing line does not exceed 30 metres and the distance between any other support and the existing line does not exceed 60 metres; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] that where the line is installed in a different position from the existing line, the existing line is removed within twelve months from the date on which the installation of the line which replaces it is completed. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Development is permitted in the case of any Class A(d) development subject to the conditions that— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] at the end of a period of six months from the date on which the installation is completed or on the ending of the diversion (whichever is the sooner) the devolved associated line is removed; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the land on which any operations have been carried out is restored as soon as reasonably practicable to its condition before the development took place. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Where it is necessary to make emergency repairs to an existing devolved associated line in a National Park or an area of outstanding natural beauty— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the limitation in paragraph A.1.(1)(b) does not apply; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] development is permitted subject to the condition that the person making those emergency repairs must notify the local planning authority as soon as practicable that those repairs have been, are being or will be made. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A.3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation of Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] For the purposes of this Part— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “devolved associated line” means an electric line which— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (a) is above ground; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (b) has a nominal voltage of 132 kilovolts or less, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (c) is associated with the construction or extension of a devolved Welsh generating station granted planning permission or consented to on or after 1 April 2019; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “devolved Welsh generating station” has the same meaning as in section 37(2B) of the Electricity Act 1989; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “electric line” has the meaning assigned to that term by section 64(1) of the Electricity Act 1989 (interpretation etc. of Part 1); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “European site” has the same meaning as in regulation 8 of the Conservation of Habitats and Species Regulations 2017); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “an existing line” means an electric line which— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (a) has been installed or is kept installed above ground in accordance with a consent granted under section 37(1) of the Electricity Act 1989 or an order granting development consent under the Planning Act 2008 or planning permission; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (b) has been installed above ground and is an electric line to which section 37(1) of the Electricity Act 1989 does not apply by virtue of paragraph 5(4) or (5) of Schedule 17 to that Act; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “small support” means a support for an electric line which does not exceed 10 metres in height. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] For the purposes of Class A(d) development “maximum distance” means— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in relation to a devolved associated line which has a nominal voltage less than 66 kilovolts, 500 metres; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in relation to any other devolved associated line, 850 metres. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] For the purposes of paragraph A.2(1)(c), any reference to the distance between a support and an existing line is a reference to the shortest distance between the centre of the base of that support and an imaginary line through the centre of the base of each support for the existing line. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

### PART 18 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — AVIATION DEVELOPMENT (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class A Development at an airport (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**The carrying out on operational land by a relevant airport operator or its agent of development (including the erection or alteration of an operational building) in connection with the provision of services and facilities at a relevant airport. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class A if it would consist of or include— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the construction or extension of a runway; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the construction of a passenger terminal the floor space of which would exceed 500 square metres; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the extension or alteration of a passenger terminal, where the floor space of the building as existing at 5th December 1988 or, if built after that date, of the building as built, would be exceeded by more than 15%; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the erection of a building other than an operational building; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the alteration or reconstruction of a building other than an operational building, where its design or external appearance would be materially affected. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Condition (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class A subject to the condition that the relevant airport operator consults the local planning authority before carrying out any development, unless that development falls within the description in paragraph A.4. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).3 Interpretation of Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of paragraph A.1, floor space shall be calculated by external measurement and without taking account of the floor space in any pier or satellite. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).4**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Development falls within this paragraph if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it is urgently required for the efficient running of the airport, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it consists of the carrying out of works, or the erection or construction of a structure or of an ancillary building, or the placing on land of equipment, and the works, structure, building, or equipment do not exceed 4 metres in height or 200 cubic metres in capacity. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would consist of the installation or erection of any radar or radio mast, antenna or other apparatus which would exceed 15 metres in height, or, where an existing mast, antenna or apparatus is replaced, the height of that mast antenna or apparatus, if greater. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “relevant airport operator" means a relevant airport operator within the meaning of section 57 of the Airports Act 1986 (scope of Part V). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] if the principal purpose of the development would be any purpose other than— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] purposes in connection with the winning and working of minerals at that mine or of minerals brought to the surface at that mine; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the treatment, storage or removal from the mine of such minerals or waste materials derived from them; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] if the external appearance of the mine would be materially affected; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] if the height of any building, plant or machinery which is not in an excavation would exceed— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] 15 metres above ground level; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height of the building, plant or machinery, if any, which is being rearranged, replaced or repaired or otherwise altered, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  whichever is the greater; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] if the height of any building, plant or machinery in an excavation would exceed— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] 15 metres above the excavated ground level; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] 15 metres above the lowest point of the unexcavated ground immediately adjacent to the excavation; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height of the building, plant or machinery, if any, which is being rearranged, replaced or repaired or otherwise altered, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  whichever is the greatest; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] if any building erected (other than a replacement building) would have a floor space exceeding 1,000 square metres; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (g (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] if the cubic content of any replaced, extended or altered building would exceed by more than 25% the cubic content of the building replaced, extended or altered or the floor space would exceed by more than 1,000 square metres the floor space of that building. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class A subject to the condition that before the end of the period of 24 months from the date when the mining operations have permanently ceased, or any longer period which the mineral planning authority agree in writing— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] on land which is not an approved site; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] on land to which the description in paragraph D.1(b) applies, unless a plan of that land was deposited with the mineral planning authority before 5th June 1989; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] if the principal purpose of the development would be any purpose other than— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] purposes in connection with the operation of the mine, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the treatment, preparation for sale, consumption or utilization of minerals won or brought to the surface at that mine, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the storage or removal from the mine of such minerals, their products or waste materials derived from them. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The prior approval referred to in Class B shall not be refused or granted subject to conditions unless the authority are satisfied that it is expedient to do so because— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the proposed development would injure the amenity of the neighbourhood and modifications can reasonably be made or conditions reasonably imposed in order to avoid or reduce that injury, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the proposed development ought to be, and could reasonably be, sited elsewhere. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
