##### C.2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Prior approvals (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The prior approval of the mineral planning authority to development permitted by Class C is not required if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the external appearance of the mine or disused mine at or adjacent to which the development is to be carried out would not be materially affected; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] no building, plant, machinery, structure or erection— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] would exceed a height of 15 metres above ground level, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where any building, plant, machinery, structure or erection is rearranged, replaced or repaired, would exceed a height of 15 metres above ground level or the height of what was rearranged, replaced or repaired, whichever is the greater, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development consists of the extension, alteration or replacement of an existing building, within the limits set out in paragraph (3). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The approval referred to in Class C shall not be refused or granted subject to conditions unless the authority are satisfied that it is expedient to do so because— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the proposed development would injure the amenity of the neighbourhood and modifications could reasonably be made or conditions reasonably imposed in order to avoid or reduce that injury, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the proposed development ought to be, and could reasonably be, sited elsewhere. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The limits referred to in paragraph C.2(1)(c) are— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] that the cubic content of the building as extended, altered or replaced does not exceed that of the existing building by more than 25%, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] that the floor space of the building as extended, altered or replaced does not exceed that of the existing building by more than 1,000 square metres. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### D (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Interpretation of Part 19 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

An area of land is an approved site for the purposes of Part 19 if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it is identified in a grant of planning permission or any instrument by virtue of which planning permission is deemed to be granted, as land which may be used for development described in this Part; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in any other case, it is land immediately adjoining an active access to an underground mine which, on 5th December 1988, was in use for the purposes of that mine, in connection with the purposes described in paragraph A.1(b)(i) or (ii) or paragraph B.1(b)(i) to (iii) above. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**D (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] For the purposes of Part 19— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “active access" means a surface access to underground workings which is in normal and regular use for the transportation of minerals, materials, spoil or men; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “ancillary mining land" means land adjacent to and occupied together with a mine at which the winning and working of minerals is carried out in pursuance of planning permission granted or deemed to be granted under Part III of the Act (control over development); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “minerals" does not include any coal other than coal won or worked during the course of operations which are carried on exclusively for the purpose of exploring for coal or confined to the digging or carrying away of coal that it is necessary to dig or carry away in the course of activities carried on for purposes which do not include the getting of coal or any product of coal; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “the prior approval of the mineral planning authority" means prior written approval of that authority of detailed proposals for the siting, design and external appearance of the building, plant or machinery proposed to be erected, installed, extended or altered; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “underground mine" is a mine at which minerals are worked principally by underground methods. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

### PART 20 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — COAL MINING DEVELOPMENT BY THE COAL AUTHORITYAND LICENSED OPERATORS (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**Development by a licensee of the Coal Authority, in a mine started before 1st July 1948, consisting of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the winning and working underground of coal or coal-related minerals in a designated seam area; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the carrying out of development underground which is required in order to gain access to and work coal or coal-related minerals in a designated seam area. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Conditions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class A subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] subject to sub-paragraph (b)— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] except in a case where there is an approved restoration scheme or mining operations have permanently ceased, the developer shall, before 31st December 1995 or before any later date which the mineral planning authority may agree in writing, apply to the mineral planning authority for approval of a restoration scheme; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where there is an approved restoration scheme, reinstatement, restoration and aftercare shall be carried out in accordance with that scheme; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] if an approved restoration scheme does not specify the periods within which reinstatement, restoration or aftercare should be carried out, it shall be subject to conditions that– *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (aa (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] reinstatement or restoration, if any, shall be carried out before the end of the period of 24 months from either the date when the mining operations have permanently ceased or the date when any application for approval of a restoration scheme under sub-paragraph (a)(i) has been finally determined, whichever is later, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (bb (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] aftercare, if any, in respect of any part of a site, shall be carried out throughout the period of five years from either the date when any reinstatement or restoration in respect of that part is completed or the date when any application for approval of a restoration scheme under sub-paragraph (a)(i) has been finally determined, whichever is later; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iv (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where there is no approved restoration scheme– *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (aa (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] all buildings, plant, machinery, structures and erections used at any time for or in connection with any previous coal-mining operations at that mine shall be removed from any land which is an authorised site unless the mineral planning authority have otherwise agreed in writing, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (bb (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] that land shall, so far as practicable, be restored to its condition before any previous coal-mining operations at that mine took place or to such condition as may have been agreed in writing between the mineral planning authority and the developer, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  before the end of the period specified in sub-paragraph (v); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  - (v (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the period referred to in sub-paragraph (iv) is– *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (aa (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the period of 24 months from the date when the mining operations have permanently ceased or, if an application for approval of a restoration scheme has been made under sub-paragraph (a)(i) before that date, 24 months from the date when that application has been finally determined, whichever is later, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (bb (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any longer period which the mineral planning authority have agreed in writing; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (vi (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] for the purposes of sub-paragraph (a), an application for approval of a restoration scheme has been finally determined when the following conditions have been met– *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (aa (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any proceedings on the application, including any proceeding on or in consequence of an application under section 288 of the Act (proceedings for questioning the validity of certain orders, decisions and directions), have been determined, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (bb (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any time for appealing under section 78 (right to appeal against planning decisions and failure to take such decisions), or applying or further applying under section 288, of the Act (where there is a right to do so) has expired; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] sub-paragraph (a) shall not apply to land in respect of which there is an extant planning permission which— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] has been granted on an application under Part III of the Act, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] has been implemented. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Interpretation of Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Class A— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “a licensee of the Coal Authority" means any person who is for the time being authorised by a licence under Part II of the Coal Industry Act 1994 to carry on coal-mining operations to which section 25 of that Act (coal-mining operations to be licensed) applies; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “approved restoration scheme" means a restoration scheme which is approved when an application made under paragraph A.1(a)(i) is finally determined, as approved (with or without conditions), or as subsequently varied with the written approval of the mineral planning authority (with or without conditions); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “coal-related minerals" means minerals other than coal which are, or may be, won and worked by coal-mining operations; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “designated seam area" means land identified, in accordance with paragraph (a) of the definition of “seam plan", in a seam plan which was deposited with the mineral planning authority before 30th September 1993; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “previous coal-mining operations" has the same meaning as in section 54(3) of the Coal Industry Act 1994 (obligations to restore land affected by coal-mining operations) and references in Class A to the use of anything in connection with any such operations shall include references to its use for or in connection with activities carried on in association with, or for purposes connected with, the carrying on of those operations; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “restoration scheme" means a scheme which makes provision for the reinstatement, restoration or aftercare (or a combination of these) of any land which is an authorised site and has been used at any time for or in connection with any previous coal-mining operations at that mine; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “seam plan" means a plan or plans on a scale of not less than 1 to 25,000 showing— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (a) land comprising the maximum extent of the coal seam or seams that could have been worked from shafts or drifts existing at a mine at 13th November 1992, without further development on an authorised site other than development permitted by Class B of Part 20 of Schedule 2 to the Town and Country Planning General Development Order 1988 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c17825731], as originally enacted; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (b) any active access used in connection with the land referred to in paragraph (a) of this definition; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (c) the National Grid lines and reference numbers shown on Ordnance Survey maps; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (d) a typical stratigraphic column showing the approximate depths of the coal seam referred to in paragraph (a) of this definition. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **Development by a licensee of the British Coal Corporation, in a mine started before 1st July 1948, consisting of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the winning and working underground of coal or coal-related minerals in a designated seam area; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the carrying out of development underground which is required in order to gain access to and work coal or coal-related minerals in a designated seam area. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Interpretation of Class B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Class B— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “designated seam area" has the same meaning as in paragraph A.2 above; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “coal-related minerals" means minerals other than coal which can only be economically worked in association with the working of coal or which can only be economically brought to the surface by the use of a mine of coal; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “a licensee of the British Coal Corporation" means any person who is for the time being authorised by virtue of section 25(3) of the Coal Industry Act 1994 (coal-mining operations to be licensed) to carry on coal-mining operations to which section 25 of that Act applies. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class C (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**C (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **Any development required for the purposes of a mine which is carried out on an authorised site at that mine by a licensed operator, in connection with coal-mining operations. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### C (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class C if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the external appearance of the mine would be materially affected; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any building, plant or machinery, structure or erection or any deposit of minerals or waste— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] would exceed a height of 15 metres above ground level, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where a building, plant or machinery would be rearranged, replaced or repaired, the resulting development would exceed a height of 15 metres above ground level or the height of what was rearranged, replaced or repaired, whichever is the greater; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any building erected (other than a replacement building) would have a floor space exceeding 1,000 square metres; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the cubic content of any replaced, extended or altered building would exceed by more than 25% the cubic content of the building replaced, extended or altered or the floor space would exceed by more than 1,000 square metres, the floor space of that building; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would be for the purpose of creating a new surface access to underground workings or of improving an existing access (which is not an active access) to underground workings; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would be carried out on land to which the description in paragraph F.2(1)(b) applies, and a plan of that land had not been deposited with the mineral planning authority before 5th June 1989. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### C (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Conditions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class C subject to the condition that before the end of the period of 24 months from the date when the mining operations have permanently ceased, or any longer period which the mineral planning authority agree in writing— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] all buildings, plant, machinery, structures and erections and deposits of minerals or waste permitted by Class C shall be removed from the land unless the mineral planning authority have otherwise agreed in writing; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the land shall, so far as is practicable, be restored to its condition before the development took place or to such condition as may have been agreed in writing between the mineral planning authority and the developer. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class D (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**D (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **Any development required for the purposes of a mine which is carried out on an authorised site at that mine by a licensed operator in connection with coal-mining operations and with the prior approval of the mineral planning authority. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### D (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class D if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would be for the purpose of creating a new surface access or improving an existing access (which is not an active access) to underground workings; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would be carried out on land to which the description in paragraph F.2(1)(b) applies, and a plan of that land had not been deposited with the mineral planning authority before 5th June 1989. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### D (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Condition (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class D subject to the condition that before the end of the period of 24 months from the date when the mining operations have permanently ceased, or any longer period which the mineral planning authority agree in writing— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] all buildings, plant, machinery, structures and erections and deposits of minerals or waste permitted by Class D shall be removed from the land, unless the mineral planning authority have otherwise agreed in writing; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the land shall, so far as is practicable, be restored to its condition before the development took place or to such condition as may have been agreed in writing between the mineral planning authority and the developer. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### D (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).3 Interpretation of Class D (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

The prior approval referred to in Class D shall not be refused or granted subject to conditions unless the authority are satisfied that it is expedient to do so because— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the proposed development would injure the amenity of the neighbourhood and modifications could reasonably be made or conditions reasonably imposed in order to avoid or reduce that injury, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the proposed development ought to be, and could reasonably be, sited elsewhere. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class E (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**E (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **The carrying out by the Coal Authority or a licensed operator, with the prior approval of the mineral planning authority, of development required for the maintenance or safety of a mine or a disused mine or for the purposes of ensuring the safety of the surface of the land at or adjacent to a mine or a disused mine. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### E.1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Prior approvals (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The prior approval of the mineral planning authority to development permitted by Class E is not required if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the external appearance of the mine or disused mine at or adjacent to which the development is to be carried out would not be materially affected; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] no building, plant or machinery, structure or erection— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] would exceed a height of 15 metres above ground level, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where any building, plant, machinery, structure or erection is rearranged, replaced or repaired, would exceed a height of 15 metres above ground level or the height of what was rearranged, replaced or repaired, whichever is the greater, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development consists of the extension, alteration or replacement of an existing building, within the limits set out in paragraph (3). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The approval referred to in Class E shall not be refused or granted subject to conditions unless the authority are satisfied that it is expedient to do so because— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the proposed development would injure the amenity of the neighbourhood and modifications could reasonably be made or conditions reasonably imposed in order to avoid or reduce that injury, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the proposed development ought to be, and could reasonably be, sited elsewhere. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The limits referred to in paragraph E.1(1)(c) are— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] that the cubic content of the building as extended, altered or replaced does not exceed that of the existing building by more than 25%, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] that the floor space of the building as extended, altered or replaced does not exceed that of the existing building by more than 1,000 square metres. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### F (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Interpretation of Part 20 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Part 20— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “active access" means a surface access to underground workings which is in normal and regular use for the transportation of coal, materials, spoil or men; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “coal-mining operations" has the same meaning as in section 65 of the Coal Industry Act 1994 (interpretation) and references to any development or use in connection with coal-mining operations shall include references to development or use for or in connection with activities carried on in association with, or for purposes connected with, the carrying on of those operations; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “licensed operator" has the same meaning as in section 65 of the Coal Industry Act 1994; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “normal and regular use" means use other than intermittent visits to inspect and maintain the fabric of the mine or any plant or machinery; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “prior approval of the mineral planning authority" means prior written approval of that authority of detailed proposals for the siting, design and external appearance of the proposed building, plant or machinery, structure or erection as erected, installed, extended or altered. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**F.2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] (1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Subject to sub-paragraph (2), land is an authorised site for the purposes of Part 20 if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it is identified in a grant of planning permission or any instrument by virtue of which planning permission is deemed to be granted as land which may be used for development described in this Part; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in any other case, it is land immediately adjoining an active access which, on 5th December 1988, was in use for the purposes of that mine in connection with coal-mining operations. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] For the purposes of sub-paragraph (1), land is not to be regarded as in use in connection with coal-mining operations if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it is used for the permanent deposit of waste derived from the winning and working of minerals; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] there is on, over or under it a railway, conveyor, aerial ropeway, roadway, overhead power line or pipe-line which is not itself surrounded by other land used for those purposes. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

### PART 21 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — WASTE TIPPING AT A MINE (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **The deposit, on premises used as a mine or on ancillary mining land already used for the purpose, of waste derived from the winning and working of minerals at that mine or from minerals brought to the surface at that mine, or from the treatment or the preparation for sale, consumption or utilization of minerals from the mine. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class A if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of waste deposited in an excavation, waste would be deposited at a height above the level of the land adjoining the excavation, unless that is provided for in a waste management scheme or a relevant scheme; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in any other case, the superficial area or height of the deposit (measured as at 21st October 1988) would be increased by more than 10%, unless such an increase is provided for in a waste management scheme or in a relevant scheme. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Conditions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class A subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] except in a case where a relevant scheme or a waste management scheme has already been approved by the mineral planning authority, the developer shall, if the mineral planning authority so require, within three months or such longer period as the authority may specify, submit a waste management scheme for that authority’s approval; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where a waste management scheme or a relevant scheme has been approved, the depositing of waste and all other activities in relation to that deposit shall be carried out in accordance with the scheme as approved. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).3 Interpretation of Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Class A— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “ancillary mining land" means land adjacent to and occupied together with a mine at which the winning and working of minerals is carried out in pursuance of planning permission granted or deemed to be granted under Part III of the Act (control over development); and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “waste management scheme" means a scheme required by the mineral planning authority to be submitted for their approval in accordance with the condition in paragraph A.2(a) which makes provision for— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (a) the manner in which the depositing of waste (other than waste deposited on a site for use for filling any mineral excavation in the mine or on ancillary mining land in order to comply with the terms of any planning permission granted on an application or deemed to be granted under Part III of the Act) is to be carried out after the date of the approval of that scheme; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (b) where appropriate, the stripping and storage of the subsoil and topsoil; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (c) the restoration and aftercare of the site. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **The deposit on land comprised in a site used for the deposit of waste materials or refuse on 1st July 1948 of waste resulting from coal-mining operations. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class B unless it is in accordance with a relevant scheme approved by the mineral planning authority before 5th December 1988. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Interpretation of Class B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Class B— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

“coal-mining operations" has the same meaning as in section 65 of the Coal Industry Act 1994 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c17825741](interpretation). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### C (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation of Part 21 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Part 21— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

“relevant scheme" means a scheme, other than a waste management scheme, requiring approval by the mineral planning authority in accordance with a condition or limitation on any planning permission granted or deemed to be granted under Part III of the Act (control over development), for making provision for the manner in which the deposit of waste is to be carried out and for the carrying out of other activities in relation to that deposit. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

### PART 22 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — MINERAL EXPLORATION (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **Development on any land during a period not exceeding 28 consecutive days consisting of– *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the drilling of boreholes; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the carrying out of seismic surveys; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the making of other excavations, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

**for the purpose of mineral exploration, and the provision or assembly on that land or adjoining land of any structure required in connection with any of those operations. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class A if– *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it consists of the drilling of boreholes for petroleum exploration; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any operation would be carried out within 50 metres of any part of an occupied residential building or a building occupied as a hospital or school; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any operation would be carried out within a National Park, an area of outstanding natural beauty, a site of archaeological interest or a site of special scientific interest; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any explosive charge of more than 1 kilogram would be used; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any excavation referred to in paragraph A(c) would exceed 10 metres in depth or 12 square metres in surface area; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case described in paragraph A(c) more than 10 excavations would, as a result, be made within any area of 1 hectare within the land during any period of 24 months; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (g (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any structure assembled or provided would exceed 12 metres in height, or, where the structure would be within 3 kilometres of the perimeter of an aerodrome, 3 metres in height. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Conditions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class A subject to the following conditions– *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] no operations shall be carried out between 6.00 p.m. and 7.00 a.m.; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] no trees on the land shall be removed, felled, lopped or topped and no other thing shall be done on the land likely to harm or damage any trees, unless the mineral planning authority have so agreed in writing; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] before any excavation (other than a borehole) is made, any topsoil and any subsoil shall be separately removed from the land to be excavated and stored separately from other excavated material and from each other; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] within a period of 28 days from the cessation of operations unless the mineral planning authority have agreed otherwise in writing– *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any structure permitted by Class A and any waste material arising from other development so permitted shall be removed from the land, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any borehole shall be adequately sealed, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any other excavation shall be filled with material from the site, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iv (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the surface of the land on which any operations have been carried out shall be levelled and any topsoil replaced as the uppermost layer, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (v (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the land shall, so far as is practicable, be restored to its condition before the development took place, including the carrying out of any necessary seeding and replanting. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **Development on any land consisting of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the drilling of boreholes; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the carrying out of seismic surveys; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **the making of other excavations, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

**for the purposes of mineral exploration, and the provision or assembly on that land or on adjoining land of any structure required in connection with any of those operations. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class B if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it consists of the drilling of boreholes for petroleum exploration; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the developer has not previously notified the mineral planning authority in writing of his intention to carry out the development (specifying the nature and location of the development); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the relevant period has not elapsed; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any explosive charge of more than 2 kilograms would be used; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any excavation referred to in paragraph B(c) would exceed 10 metres in depth or 12 square metres in surface area; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any structure assembled or provided would exceed 12 metres in height. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).2 Conditions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class B subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development shall be carried out in accordance with the details in the notification referred to in paragraph B.1(b), unless the mineral planning authority have otherwise agreed in writing; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] no trees on the land shall be removed, felled, lopped or topped and no other thing shall be done on the land likely to harm or damage any trees, unless specified in detail in the notification refered to in paragraph B.1(b) or the mineral planning authority have otherwise agreed in writing; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] before any excavation other than a borehole is made, any topsoil and any subsoil shall be separately removed from the land to be excavated and stored separately from other excavated material and from each other; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class B if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the developer has not previously notified the mineral planning authority in writing of his intention to carry out the development and supplied them with the appropriate details; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the deposit covers a ground area exceeding 2 hectares, unless the deposit contains no mineral or other material which was deposited on the land more than 5 years before the development; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the deposit derives from the carrying out of any operations permitted under Part 6 of this Schedule or any Class in a previous development order which it replaces. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class B subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it shall be carried out in accordance with the details given in the notice sent to the mineral planning authority referred to in paragraph B.1(a) above, unless that authority have agreed otherwise in writing; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] if the mineral planning authority so require, the developer shall within a period of three months from the date of the requirement (or such other longer period as that authority may provide) submit to them for approval a scheme providing for the restoration and aftercare of the site; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where such a scheme is required, the site shall be restored and aftercare shall be carried out in accordance with the provisions of the approved scheme; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] development shall not be commenced until the relevant period has elapsed. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “relevant period" means the period elapsing— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (a) where a direction is not issued under article 7, 28 days after the notification referred to in paragraph B.1(a) or, if earlier, on the date on which the mineral planning authority notify the developer in writing that they will not issue such a direction; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (b) where a direction is issued under article 7, 28 days from the date on which notice of that direction is sent to the Secretary of State, or, if earlier, the date on which the mineral planning authority notify the developer that the Secretary of State has disallowed the direction. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
