##### A.2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Conditions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Class A(a) and Class A(c) development is permitted subject to the condition that any antenna or supporting apparatus, radio equipment housing or development ancillary to radio equipment housing installed, altered or replaced on a building in accordance with that permission must, so far as is practicable, be sited so as to minimise its effect on the external appearance of the building. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Class A(a) and Class A(c) development is permitted subject to the condition that any apparatus or structure provided in accordance with that permission must be removed from the land, building or structure on which it is situated— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] if such development was carried out in an emergency on any protected land, at the expiry of the relevant period, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in any other case, as soon as reasonably practicable after it is no longer required for any electronic communications purposes, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

and such land, building or structure must be restored to its condition before the development took place or to any other condition as may be agreed in writing between the local planning authority and the developer. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Class A(b) development is permitted subject to the condition that any apparatus or structure provided in accordance with that permission must be removed from the land at the expiry of the relevant period and the land restored to its condition before the development took place. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Except in relation to development described in paragraph (5) and subject to paragraph (7), class A development on— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] protected land, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] unprotected land consisting of the installation, alteration or replacement of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a mast; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] an antenna on a building or structure (other than a mast) where the antenna (including any supporting structure) would exceed the height of the building or structure at the point where it is installed or to be installed by six metres or more; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a public call box; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iv (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] radio equipment housing, where the volume of any single development is in excess of 2.5 cubic metres, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  is permitted subject, except in case of emergency, to the conditions set out in A.3. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Class A development on any article 1(5) land, which consists of the installation, alteration or replacement of a telegraph pole, cabinet or line, in connection with the provision of fixed-line broadband, is permitted, subject to the conditions set out in paragraph (6). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(6 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The conditions are— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the developer must give one month’s notice, in writing, to the relevant local planning authority and to the Natural Resources Body for Wales where the development, or any part of it, is in— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a National Park, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] an area of outstanding natural beauty; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the notice to be given under paragraph (a) must state the developer’s intention to install electronic communications apparatus, describe the apparatus and identify the location where it is proposed to install it; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any cabinet must be: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] green; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] black (except matt black); or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a colour which has the written approval of the local planning authority prior to the commencement of the development; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any telegraph pole must have the same appearance and be made of the same material as the nearest existing telegraph pole to it which has planning permission, unless an alternative appearance or material has been approved in writing by the local planning authority prior to the commencement of the development. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(7 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Paragraph (4) does not apply to development consisting of the alteration or replacement of a mast— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] on any protected land which excluding any antenna would not, when altered or replaced, exceed the greater of the height of the existing mast and 15 metres above ground level; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] on unprotected land which excluding any antenna would not, when altered or replaced, exceed the greater of the height of the existing mast and 20 metres above ground level. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A.3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Prior approval (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The developer must give notice of the proposed development to any person (other than the developer) who is an owner of the land to which the development relates, or a tenant, before making the application required by paragraph (3)— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] by serving a developer’s notice on every such person whose name and address is known to the developer; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where the developer has taken reasonable steps to ascertain the names and addresses of every such person, but has been unable to do so, by local advertisement. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Where the proposed development consists of the installation of a mast within three kilometres of the perimeter of an aerodrome, the developer must notify the Civil Aviation Authority, the Secretary of State for Defence or the aerodrome operator, as appropriate, before making the application required by paragraph (3). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Before beginning the development, the developer must apply to the local planning authority for a determination as to whether the prior approval of the authority will be required to the siting and appearance of the development. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The application must be accompanied by– *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a written description of the proposed development and a plan indicating its proposed location together with any fee required to be paid; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the developer’s contact address, and the developer’s email address if the developer has one; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] if the development involves the installation of one or more antennas, unless they are all *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Regulation 2020/1070 small cell systems or small cell systems,[^c-9b509d7e8525ef20a2141e0e8db743db] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a written declaration that the equipment and installation to which the application relates is so designed that it will, when installed, operate, having regard to its location and the manner in which it has been installed, in full compliance with the requirements of the radio frequency public exposure guidelines of the International Commission on Non-ionising Radiation Protection *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [^c-a04d9d6c5abeb94ec641ce9de7762726]... *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where paragraph (1) applies, by evidence that the requirements of paragraph (1) have been satisfied; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where paragraph (2) applies, by evidence that the Civil Aviation Authority, the Secretary of State for Defence or the aerodrome operator, as the case may be, has been notified of the proposal. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Subject to paragraphs (7)(c) and (d), upon receipt of the application under paragraph (4) the local planning authority must— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] for development which, in their opinion, falls within a category set out in the table of schedule 4 to the Procedure Order, consult the authority or person mentioned in relation to that category, except where– *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the local planning authority are the authority so mentioned; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the authority or person so mentioned has advised the local planning authority that they do not wish to be consulted, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  and give the consultees at least 14 days within which to comment; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of development which does not accord with the provisions of the development plan in force in the area in which the land to which the application relates is situated or which would affect a right of way to which Part 3 of the Wildlife and Countryside Act 1981 (public rights of way) applies, give notice of the proposed development, in the appropriate form set out in Schedule 3 to the Procedure Order— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] by site display in at least one place on or near the land to which the application relates for not less than 21 days and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] by local advertisement; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of development which does not fall within paragraph (b) but which involves development carried out on a site having an area of one hectare or more, give notice of the proposed development, in the appropriate form set out in Schedule 3 to the Procedure Order— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] by site display in least one place on or near the land to which the application relates for not less than 21 days, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] by serving notice on any adjoining owner or occupier, and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] by local advertisement; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of development which does not fall within (b) or (c), give notice of the proposed development, in the appropriate form set out in Schedule 3 to the Procedure Order— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] by site display in at least one place on or near the land to which the application relates for not less than 21 days, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] by serving the notice on any adjoining owner or occupier. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(6 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The local planning authority must take into account any representations made to them as a result of consultations or notices given under A.3(5), when determining the application made under paragraph (3). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(7 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The development must not be begun before the occurrence of one of the following— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the receipt by the applicant from the local planning authority of a written notice of their determination that such prior approval is not required; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where the local planning authority gives the applicant written notice that such prior approval is required, the giving of that approval to the applicant, in writing, within a period of 56 days beginning with the date on which they received the application; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where the local planning authority gives the applicant written notice that such prior approval is required, the expiry of a period of 56 days beginning with the date on which the authority received the application without the authority notifying the applicant, in writing, that such approval is given or refused; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the expiry of a period of 56 days beginning with the date on which the local planning authority received the application without the authority notifying the applicant, in writing, of their determination as to whether such prior approval is required. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(8 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The development must, except to the extent that the local planning authority otherwise agree in writing, be carried out– *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where prior approval has been given as mentioned in paragraph (7)(b) in accordance with the details approved; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in any other case, in accordance with the details submitted with the application. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(9 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The agreement in writing referred to in paragraph (8) requires no special form of writing, and in particular there is no requirement on the developer to submit a new application for prior approval in the case of minor amendments to the details submitted with the application for prior approval. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(10 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The development must be begun— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where prior approval has been given as mentioned in paragraph (7)(b), not later than the expiration of five years beginning with the date on which the approval was given; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in any other case, not later than the expiration of five years beginning with the date on which the local planning authority were given the information referred to in paragraph (4). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(11 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] In a case of emergency, development is permitted by Class A subject to the condition that the operator must give written notice to the local planning authority of such development as soon as possible after the emergency begins. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A.4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation of Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] For the purposes of Class A— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “aerodrome operator” means the person for the time being having the management of an aerodrome or, in relation to a particular aerodrome, the management of that aerodrome; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “antenna system” means a set of antennas installed on a building or structure and operated in accordance with the electronic communications code; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “Commission Regulation 2020/1070” means Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) 2020/1070 on specifying the characteristics of small-area wireless access points pursuant to Article 57 paragraph 2 of the Directive;[^c-31afb8d21dc8d84ee3e1a805f9d472c4] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “development ancillary to radio equipment housing” means the installation, alteration or replacement of structures, equipment or means of access which are ancillary to and reasonably required for the purposes of the radio equipment housing, and except on any land which is, or is within, a site of special scientific interest includes— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (a) security equipment; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (b) perimeter walls and fences; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (c) handrails, steps and ramps; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “developer’s notice” means a notice signed and dated by or on behalf of the developer and containing— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (a) the name of the developer; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (b) the address or location of the proposed development; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (c) a description of the proposed development (including its siting and appearance and the height of any mast); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (d) a statement that the developer will apply to the local planning authority for a determination as to whether the prior approval of the authority will be required to the siting and appearance of the development; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (e) the name and address of the local planning authority to whom the application will be made; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (f) a statement that the application must be available for public inspection at the offices of the local planning authority during usual office hours; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (g) a statement that any person who wishes to make representations about the siting and appearance of the proposed development may do so in writing to the local planning authority; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (h) the date by which any such representations should be received by the local planning authority, being a date not less than 14 days from the date of the notice; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i) the address to which such representations should be made. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “the Directive” means Directive (EU) 2018/1972 of the European Parliament and Council establishing the European Electronic Communications Code (recast); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “electronic communications apparatus”, “electronic communications code”, “electronic communications network” and “electronic communications service” have the same meaning as in the Communications Act 2003; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “existing electronic communications apparatus” means electronic communications apparatus which is already sending or receiving electronic communications;” *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “fixed-line broadband” means a service or connection (commonly referred to as being ‘always on’), via a fixed-line network, providing a bandwidth greater than narrowband; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “land controlled by the operator” means land occupied by the operator in right of a freehold interest or a leasehold interest under a lease granted for a term of not less than 10 years; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “local advertisement” means by publication of the notice in a newspaper circulating in the locality in which the land to which the application relates is situated; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “mast” means a radio mast or a radio tower; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “narrowband” means a service or connection providing data speeds up to 128 k bit/s; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “owner” means any person who is the estate owner in respect of the fee simple, or who is entitled to a tenancy granted or extended for a term of years certain of which not less than seven years remain unexpired; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “protected land” means any land which is article 1(5) land or land which is, or is within, a site of special scientific interest; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  - (a) to which Commission Regulation 2020/1070 applies; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “relevant period” means a period which expires– *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (a) six months from the commencement of the installation, alteration or replacement of any apparatus or structure permitted by Class A(a) or Class A(c); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (b) eighteen months from the commencement of the use permitted by Class A(b);or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (c) when the need for such apparatus, structure or use ceases, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

    whichever occurs first; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “small cell system” means an antenna which may be referred to as a femtocell, picocell, metrocell or microcell antenna, together with any ancillary apparatus, which— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  - (c) does not have a volume exceeding 50,000 cubic centimetres; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  but does not include a Regulation 2020/1070 small cell system and any calculation for the purposes of paragraph (b) or (c) includes any power supply unit or casing, but excludes any mounting, fixing, bracket or other support structure;. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “unprotected land” means any land which is not protected land. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).1 Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^M_F_ebaa325f-5243-40b9-cc6c-407103188e21][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class A if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the building is a dwellinghouse or the building or structure is within the curtilage of a dwellinghouse; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would consist of development of a kind described in paragraph A of Part 24; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would consist of the installation, alteration or replacement of system apparatus within the meaning of section 8(6) of the Road Traffic (Driver Licensing and Information Systems) Act 1989 (definitions of driver information systems *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] etc *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would result in the presence on the building or structure of more than four antennas; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of an antenna installed on a chimney, the length of the antenna would exceed 60cm; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in all other cases, the length of the antenna would exceed 130cm; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (g (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would consist of the installation of an antenna with a cubic capacity in excess of 35 litres; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (h (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the highest part of the antenna or its supporting structure would be more than three metres higher than the highest part of the building or structure on which it is installed or is to be installed; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of article 1(5) land, it would consist of the installation of an antenna on a chimney, wall or roof slope which faces onto, and is visible from, a highway. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (b (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-9d27815336f7b64aa8c4d6bf75fcd392][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] an antenna no longer needed for reception or transmission purposes shall be removed from the building or structure as soon as reasonably practicable *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**B.3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^M_F_d81b665c-6ece-4a2e-e717-8f4cc2aac92f][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] B.3 The relevant size criteria for the purposes of paragraph B.1(d) are that— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] only one of the antennas may exceed 60 centimetres in length; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any antenna which exceeds 60 centimetres in length must not exceed 100 centimetres in length. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**B.4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The length of an antenna is to be measured in any linear direction and shall exclude any projecting feed element, reinforcing rim, mounting or brackets. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**B.5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The maximum number of antenna for the purpose of paragraph B.1(d) includes any *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] small cell system[^c-3fee7b11021bc7088df5a5a5d7789f0b] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] permitted under Class A of Part 24 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] , but excludes Regulation 2010/1070 small cell systems to the extent that they are permitted by paragraph A.1(q) of Part 24[^c-0f852732002259a2847754e0dcb282ec] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**The use of land by members of a recreational organisation for the purposes of recreation or instruction, and the erection or placing of tents on the land for the purposes of the use. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
