##### A (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

The erection, construction, maintenance, improvement or alteration of a gate, fence, wall of other means of enclosure by or on behalf of the Crown on Crown land for national security purposes. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A.1 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Development not permitted (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class A if the height of any gate, fence, wall or other means of enclosure erected or constructed would exceed 4.5 metres above ground level. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class B (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### B (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

The installation, alteration or replacement by or on behalf of the Crown on Crown land of a closed circuit television camera and associated lighting for national security purposes. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### B.1 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Development not permitted (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class B if— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the dimensions of the camera including its housing exceed 75 centimetres by 25 centimetres by 25 centimetres; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the uniform level of lighting provided exceeds 10 lux measured at ground level. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### B.2 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Conditions (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class B subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the camera must, so far as practicable, be sited so as to minimise its effect on the external appearance of any building to which it is fixed; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the camera must be removed as soon as reasonably practicable after it is no longer required for national security purposes. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### B.3 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation of Class B (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Class B— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “camera” except in paragraph B1(a) includes its housing, pan and tilt mechanism, infra red illuminator, receiver, mountings and brackets; and *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “ground level” means the level of the surface of the ground immediately adjacent to the building to which the camera is attached or, where the level of the surface of the ground is not uniform, the level of the lowest part of the surface of the ground adjacent to it. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class C (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### C (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development by or on behalf of the Crown for national security purposes in, on, over or under Crown land, consisting of— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the installation, alteration or replacement of any electronic communications apparatus; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the use of land in an emergency for a period not exceeding six months to station and operate moveable electronic communications apparatus required for the replacement of unserviceable electronic communications apparatus, including the provision of moveable structures on the land for the purposes of that use; or *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] development ancillary to radio equipment housing. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### C.1 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Development not permitted (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class C(a) if— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of the installation of apparatus (other than on a building) the apparatus, excluding any antenna, would exceed a height of 15 metres above ground level; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of the alteration or replacement of apparatus already installed (other than on a building), the apparatus, excluding any antenna, would, when altered or replaced, exceed the height of the existing apparatus or a height of 15 metres above ground level, whichever is the greater; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of the installation, alteration or replacement of apparatus on a building, the height of the apparatus (taken by itself) would exceed the height of the existing apparatus or— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] 15 metres, where it is installed, or is to be installed, on a building which is 30 metres or more in height; or *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] 10 metres in any other case, *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  whichever is the greater; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of the installation, alteration or replacement of apparatus on a building, the highest part of the apparatus when installed, altered or replaced would exceed the height of the highest part of the building by more than the height of the existing apparatus or— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] 10 metres, where it is installed, or is to be installed, on a building which is 30 metres or more in height; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] 8 metres, in the case of a building which is more than 15 metres but less than 30 metres in height; or *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] 6 metres in any other case. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  whichever is the greater; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of the installation, alteration or replacement of apparatus (other than an antenna) on a mast, the height of the mast and the apparatus supported by it would, when the apparatus was installed, altered or replaced, exceed any relevant height limit specified in respect of apparatus in paragraphs C.1(a), (b), (c) and (d), and for the purposes of applying the limit specified in sub-paragraph (c), the words “(taken by itself)”must be disregarded; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (f (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of the installation, alteration or replacement of any apparatus other than— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a mast; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] an antenna; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any apparatus which does not project above the level of the surface of the ground; or *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iv (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] radio equipment housing, *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  the ground or base area of the structure would exceed the ground or base area of the existing structure or 1.5 square metres, whichever is the greater; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (g (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of the installation, alteration or replacement of an antenna on a building (other than a mast) which is less than 15 metres in height; on a mast located on such a building; or, where the antenna is to be located below a height of 15 metres above ground level, on a building (other than a mast) which is 15 metres or more in height— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the antenna is to be located on a wall or roof slope facing a highway which is within 20 metres of the building on which the antenna is to be located, unless it is essential for operational purposes that the antenna is located in that position; or *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of dish antennas, the size of any dish would exceed the size of the existing dish when measured in any dimension or 1.3 metres when measured in any dimension, whichever is the greater; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (h (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of the installation, alteration or replacement of a dish antenna on a building (other than a mast) which is 15 metres or more in height, or on a mast located on such a building , where the antenna is located at a height of 15 metres or above, measured from ground level the size of any dish would exceed the size of the existing dish when measured in any dimension or 1.3 metres when measured in any dimension, whichever is the greater; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (i (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of the installation of a mast, on a building which is less than 15 metres in height, such a mast would be within 20 metres of a highway, unless it is essential for operational purposes that the mast is installed in that position; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (j (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of the installation, alteration or replacement of radio equipment housing— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development is not ancillary to the use of any other electronic communications apparatus; or *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development would exceed 90 cubic metres or, if located on the roof of a building, the development would exceed 30 cubic metres. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**C.2 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Development consisting of the installation of apparatus is not permitted by Class C(a) on article 1(5) land unless— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the land on which the apparatus is to be installed is, or forms part of, a site on which there is existing electronic communication apparatus; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the existing apparatus was installed on the site on or before the relevant day; and *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the site was Crown land on the relevant day. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**C.3 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] (1 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Subject to paragraph (2), development is not permitted by Class C(a) if it will result in the installation of more than one item of apparatus (“the original apparatus”) on a site in addition to any item of apparatus already on that site on the relevant day. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(2 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] In addition to the original apparatus which may be installed on a site by virtue of Class C(a), for every four items of apparatus which existed on that site on the relevant day, one additional item of small apparatus may be installed. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(3 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] In paragraph (2), “small apparatus” means— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a dish antenna, other than on a building, not exceeding 5 metres in diameter and 7 metres in height; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] an antenna, other than a dish antenna and other than on a building, not exceeding 7 metres in height; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a hard standing or other base for any apparatus described in sub-paragraphs (a) and (b), not exceeding 7 metres in diameter; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a dish antenna on a building, not exceeding 1.3 metres in diameter and 3 metres in height; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] an antenna, other than a dish antenna, on a building, not exceeding 3 metres in height; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (f (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a mast on a building, not exceeding 3 metres in height; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (g (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] equipment housing not exceeding 3 metres in height and of which the area, when measured at ground level, does not exceed 9 square metres. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### C.4 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Conditions (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(1 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Class C(a) and Class C(c) development is permitted subject to the condition that any antenna or supporting apparatus, radio equipment housing or development ancillary to radio equipment housing constructed, installed, altered or replaced on a building in accordance with that permission must, so far as is practicable, be sited so as to minimise its effect on the external appearance of the building. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(2 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Class C(a) development consisting of the installation of any additional apparatus on article 1(5) land is permitted subject to the condition that the apparatus must be installed as close as is reasonably practicable to any existing apparatus. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(3 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Class C(b) development is permitted subject to the condition that any apparatus or structure provided in accordance with that permission must, at the expiry of the relevant period be removed from the land and the land restored to its condition before the development took place. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(4 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Class C development— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] on article 1(5) land or land which is, or is within, a site of special scientific interest; or *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] on any other land and consisting of the construction, installation, alteration or replacement of a mast; or of an antenna on a building or structure (other than a mast) where the antenna (including any supporting structure) would exceed the height of the building or structure at the point where it is installed or to be installed by 4 metres or more; or of radio equipment housing with a volume in excess of 2.5 cubic metres; or of development ancillary to radio equipment housing— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

is permitted subject, except in case of emergency, to the conditions set out in C.5. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**C.5 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] (1 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] The developer must, before commencing development, give notice of the proposed development to any person (other than the developer) who is an owner or tenant of the land to which the development relates— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] by serving the appropriate notice on every such person whose name and address is known to the developer; and *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where the developer has taken reasonable steps to ascertain the names and addresses of every such person, but has been unable to do so, by local advertisement. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

(2 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] Where the proposed development consists of the installation of a mast within 3 kilometres of the perimeter of an aerodrome, the developer shall, before commencing development, notify the Civil Aviation Authority, the Secretary of State for Defence or the aerodrome operator, as appropriate. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### C.6 (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation of Class C (Extent: W) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Class C— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “aerodrome operator” means the person who is for the time being responsible for the management of the aerodrome; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “development ancillary to radio equipment housing” means the construction, installation, alteration or replacement of structures, equipment or means of access which are ancillary to and reasonably required for the purposes of the radio equipment housing; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “appropriate notice” means a notice signed and dated by or on behalf of the developer and containing— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (a) the name of the developer; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (b) the address or location of the proposed development; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (c) a description of the proposed development (including its siting and appearance and the height of any mast); *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “local advertisement” means by publication of the notice in a newspaper circulating in the locality in which the land to which the proposed development relates is situated; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “mast” means a radio mast or a radio tower; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “owner” means any person who is the estate owner in respect of the fee simple, or who is entitled to a tenancy granted or extended for a term of years certain of which not less than seven years remain unexpired; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “relevant day” means— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (a) 7th June 2006; or *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (b) where apparatus is installed pursuant to planning permission granted on or after 7th June 2006, the date when that apparatus is finally installed pursuant to that permission, *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  whichever is later; *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “relevant period” means a period which expires— *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (a) six months from the commencement of the construction, installation, alteration or replacement of any apparatus or structure permitted by Class C(a) or Class C(c) or from the commencement of the use permitted by Class C(b), as the case may be; or *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (b) when the need for such apparatus, structure or use ceases, *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  whichever occurs first; and *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “tenant” means the tenant of an agricultural holding any part of which is comprised in the land to which the proposed development relates. *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

### PART 39 (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — TEMPORARY PROTECTION OF POULTRY AND OTHER CAPTIVE BIRDS (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^M_F_e420b56a-ee28-4cef-99d0-5edcb2511737][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class A (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

The erection of a building where that is necessary for the purpose of housing poultry or other captive birds to protect them from avian influenza. *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A.1 (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Development not permitted (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class A if— *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development would affect a listed building or its setting; *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the height of the building would exceed 12 metres; *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where the development is within three kilometres of an aerodrome, the height of the building would exceed three metres; *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the area of ground which would be covered by the building would exceed 465 square metres; *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where development permitted by Class A is carried out more than once on land in the occupation of a particular person, the aggregate of the area of ground covered by any such development would exceed 465 square metres; *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (f (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] where the development consists of the extension of a building, the area of ground covered by the building as extended would exceed the area of ground covered by the existing building by more than 50 per cent. *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A.2 (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Conditions (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class A subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the development shall not be used for any purpose other than to house poultry or other captive birds to protect them from avian influenza; *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the developer shall, as soon as practicable, and in any event no later than 14 days, after commencing development, serve the relevant notice on the local planning authority; and *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] as soon as practicable on or after the relevant date *(Extent: W)*[^c-edb5198a28d563919e75a7d9e4aae1e9] *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]— *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any building permitted by Class A shall be removed from the land; and *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the land shall be restored to its condition before the development took place, or restored to such other condition as may be agreed in writing between the local planning authority and the developer. *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A.3 (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation of Class A (Extent: E) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

For the purposes of Class A— *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- “approved body” means a body approved in accordance with Article 2(1)(c) of Directive *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [92/65/ *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*EEC *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1992/0065)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] laying down animal health requirements governing trade in and imports into the Community of animals, semen, ova and embryos not subject to animal health requirements laid down in specific Community rules referred to in Annex A(1) to Directive *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] [90/425/ *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*EEC *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1990/0425)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]; *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “avian influenza” means an infection of poultry or other captive birds caused by any influenza A virus of the subtypes H5 or H7 or with an intravenous pathogenicity index in six week old chickens greater than 1.2; *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “other captive bird” means a bird kept in captivity which is not poultry and includes a bird kept as a pet; for shows, races, exhibitions or competitions; for breeding; for sale; or for use by an approved body; *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “poultry” means birds reared or kept in captivity for the production of meat or eggs for consumption, for the production of other products, for restocking supplies of game or for the purposes of any breeding programme for the production of such categories of birds; *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “relevant date” means— *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (a) 21 March *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] 2009[^c-d8617dba58ff21f1f3ab8a2b4553a89c] *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]; or *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (b) the date on which the use of the building permitted by Class A ceases to be necessary for the purposes of protecting poultry or other captive birds from avian influenza, *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

  whichever is the earlier; *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- “relevant notice” means a notice signed and dated by or on behalf of the developer and containing— *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (a) the name of the developer; *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (b) the address or location of the development (including a site plan and grid reference); *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (c) the name and address of the owner and occupier of the land on which the development is being carried out (if not the developer); *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (d) a description of the development (including the type of poultry or other captive birds to be protected); and *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (e) the date on which the development commenced. *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

### PART 40 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — INSTALLATION OF DOMESTIC MICROGENERATION EQUIPMENT (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-f0628e0f7385e285a122e7c004b34a25][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

#### Class A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permitted development (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

**The installation, alteration or replacement of solar PV or solar thermal equipment on— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **a dwellinghouse; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] **a building situated within the curtilage of a dwellinghouse. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]**

##### A.1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Development not permitted (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is not permitted by Class A if— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of solar PV or solar thermal equipment installed on a wall or pitched roof— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the solar PV or solar thermal equipment would protrude more than 20 centimetres beyond the plane of the wall or the roof slope when measured from the perpendicular with the external surface of the wall or roof slope; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] it would result in the highest part of the solar PV or solar thermal equipment being higher than the highest part of the roof (excluding any chimney); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of solar PV or solar thermal equipment installed on a flat roof— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the solar PV or solar thermal equipment would be sited within 1 metre of the external edge of the roof; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the solar PV or solar thermal equipment would protrude more than 1 metre above the plane of the roof; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of land within a conservation area or a World Heritage Site, the solar PV or solar thermal equipment would be installed— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] on a wall forming the principal or side elevation of the dwellinghouse and which fronts a highway; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] on a wall of a building within the curtilage of the dwellinghouse and which fronts a highway; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the solar PV or solar thermal equipment would be installed on a building within the curtilage of the dwellinghouse if the dwellinghouse is a listed building; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the solar PV or solar thermal equipment would be installed on a site designated as a scheduled monument. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

##### A.2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Conditions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c][^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

Development is permitted by Class A subject to the following conditions— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] solar PV or solar thermal equipment must, so far as practicable, be sited so as to minimise its effect on the external appearance of the building; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] solar PV or solar thermal equipment must, so far as practicable, be sited so as to minimise its effect on the amenity of the area; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] solar PV or solar thermal equipment no longer needed for or capable of microgeneration must be removed as soon as reasonably practicable. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] in the case of the installation of stand alone solar, it would result in the presence within the curtilage of more than one stand alone solar; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] any part of the stand alone solar— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] would exceed four metres in height; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] would be installed within five metres of the boundary of the curtilage of the dwellinghouse and would— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (aa (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] exceed two metres in height; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
    - (bb (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] be installed within five metres of a highway; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] would, in the case of land within a conservation area or a World Heritage Site, be installed so that it is between a highway which bounds the curtilage and the dwellinghouse; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (iv (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] would be installed within the curtilage of a listed building; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] the surface area of the solar panels forming part of the stand alone solar would exceed nine square metres or any dimension of its array (including any housing) would exceed three metres. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]

    - (bb (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] a block of flats; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c] more than two air source heat pumps on, or within the curtilage of, a detached dwellinghouse; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0b7983d6c3350cb737b2504f32c7393c]
